# Lab8 Client (JavaFX)

This module provides the JavaFX client UI. The login screen is wired to the existing backend auth flow by sending the `__get_commands__` request with provided credentials.

## Run

Build the client module:

```powershell
mvn -pl client -am test
```

To launch the UI, run the `client.app.JavaFxLauncher` main class from your IDE or provide a JavaFX runtime on the command line:

```powershell
java -cp client/target/classes;common/target/classes client.app.JavaFxLauncher localhost 5000
```

If you use a JavaFX SDK outside the JDK, add the JavaFX modules to the `--module-path` and `--add-modules` options.

package client.app;

import client.ClientCommandRegistry;
import client.NonBlockingTcpClient;
import client.ScriptExecutor;
import client.commands.CommandDispatcher;
import client.commands.CommandExecutionContext;
import client.ui.ClientUI;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandRequest;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import common.network.AuthCredentials;
import common.network.CommandMeta;
import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.ProtocolConstants;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;
import java.util.Map;

public class ClientApplication {
    private final String host;
    private final int port;
    private final ClientUI ui;

    public ClientApplication(String host, int port, ClientUI ui) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
        this.ui = ui;
    }

    public void start() {
        AuthCredentials credentials = ui.promptCredentials();
        if (credentials == null) return;

        ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry = new ClientCommandRegistry();
        NonBlockingTcpClient tcpClient = new NonBlockingTcpClient(host, port);

        if (!bootstrapCommands(commandRegistry, tcpClient, credentials)) return;

        ui.println("Client mode. Server: " + host + ":" + port);
        ui.println("Type 'help' for commands.");

        ScriptExecutor scriptExecutor = new ScriptExecutor(commandRegistry, tcpClient, ui);
        CommandExecutionContext context = new CommandExecutionContext(
                commandRegistry, tcpClient,
                ui::promptMusicBand,
                scriptExecutor::executeScript,
                true, false, 3, ui
        );
        context.setCredentials(credentials);

        CommandDispatcher dispatcher = new CommandDispatcher(context);
        try {
            ui.startInteractiveLoop(dispatcher);
        } finally {
            ui.close();
        }
    }

    private boolean bootstrapCommands(ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry,
                                      NonBlockingTcpClient tcpClient,
                                      AuthCredentials credentials) {
        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
            try {
                CommandPacket packet = new CommandPacket(
                        ProtocolConstants.GET_COMMANDS,
                        new CommandRequest("", credentials));
                ResponsePacket responsePacket = tcpClient.send(packet, 15000);
                CommandResult result = responsePacket.getResult();
                if (!result.isSuccess()) {
                    ui.printError("Failed to load command metadata: " + result.getMessage());
                    return false;
                }
                if (!(result.getData() instanceof Map<?, ?> rawMap)) {
                    ui.printError("Server returned invalid metadata format.");
                    return false;
                }
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                Map<String, CommandMeta> commandMap = (Map<String, CommandMeta>) rawMap;
                commandRegistry.applyServerCommands(commandMap);
                return true;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                if (attempt == 3) {
                    ui.printError("Cannot connect to server to load command metadata: " + e.getMessage());
                    return false;
                }
                ui.printError("Server temporarily unavailable while loading command metadata. Retrying...");
                try { Thread.sleep(500); } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

package client.app;

import client.ui.ClientUI;
import client.ui.ConsoleClientUI;

public class ClientMain {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        String host = args.length > 0 ? args[0] : "localhost";
        int port = 5000;
        if (args.length > 1) {
            try {
                port = Integer.parseInt(args[1]);
                if (port <= 0 || port > 65535) throw new NumberFormatException("out of range");
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                System.err.println("Invalid port: " + args[1] + ". Use value in range 1..65535.");
                return;
            }
        }
        ClientUI ui = new ConsoleClientUI();
        new ClientApplication(host, port, ui).start();
    }
}

package client.app;

import client.javafx.MainController;
import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.LoginController;
import javafx.application.Application;
import javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader;
import javafx.scene.Parent;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.stage.Stage;

import java.io.IOException;

public class JavaFxApp extends Application {
    private AppState appState;
    private Stage primaryStage;

    @Override
    public void init() {
        String host = "localhost";
        int port = 5000;
        if (!getParameters().getRaw().isEmpty()) {
            host = getParameters().getRaw().get(0);
        }
        if (getParameters().getRaw().size() > 1) {
            try {
                port = Integer.parseInt(getParameters().getRaw().get(1));
                if (port <= 0 || port > 65535) throw new NumberFormatException("out of range");
            } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
                port = 5000;
            }
        }
        this.appState = new AppState(host, port);
    }

    @Override
    public void start(Stage stage) throws IOException {
        this.primaryStage = stage;
        showLogin();
        stage.setTitle("BandManager");
        stage.show();
    }

    private void showLogin() throws IOException {
        FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/fxml/LoginView.fxml"));
        Parent root = loader.load();
        LoginController controller = loader.getController();
        controller.init(appState);

        Scene scene = new Scene(root, 1024, 768);
        primaryStage.setScene(scene);

        appState.credentialsProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
            if (newVal != null && oldVal == null) {
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> {
                    try {
                        showMain();
                    } catch (IOException e) {
                        e.printStackTrace();
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    }

    private void showMain() throws IOException {
        FXMLLoader loader = new FXMLLoader(getClass().getResource("/fxml/MainView.fxml"));
        Parent root = loader.load();
        MainController controller = loader.getController();
        controller.init(appState);

        Scene scene = new Scene(root, 1280, 800);
        primaryStage.setScene(scene);
    }

    @Override
    public void stop() {
        if (appState != null) {
            appState.shutdown();
        }
    }
}

package client;

import common.network.CommandMeta;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class ClientCommandRegistry {
    private final Map<String, CommandMeta> commands = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    public ClientCommandRegistry() {
        // Local commands that dont need server
        commands.put("execute_script", new CommandMeta("execute_script <file_name>", "Execute commands from script", true, false));
        commands.put("exit", new CommandMeta("exit", "Exit client application", false, false));
    }

    public void applyServerCommands(Map<String, CommandMeta> serverCommands) {
        commands.entrySet().removeIf(entry -> !isClientLocal(entry.getKey()));
        if (serverCommands != null) {
            serverCommands.forEach(commands::putIfAbsent);
        }
    }

    private boolean isClientLocal(String commandName) {
        return "execute_script".equals(commandName) || "exit".equals(commandName);
    }

    public CommandMeta get(String commandName) {
        return commands.get(commandName);
    }

    public List<CommandMeta> exportPublicCommandMeta() {
        List<CommandMeta> list = new ArrayList<>();
        commands.forEach((name, meta) -> {
            if (name == null || name.startsWith("__")) return;
            list.add(new CommandMeta(meta.usage(), meta.description(), meta.requiresArgument(), meta.requiresBand()));
        });
        return list;
    }

    public String buildHelpText() {
        StringBuilder help = new StringBuilder("Available commands:\n");
        commands.forEach((name, meta) -> help.append(" ")
                .append(name)
                .append(" - ")
                .append(meta.description())
                .append("\n    Usage: ")
                .append(meta.usage())
                .append("\n"));
        return help.toString();
    }
}

package client.commands;

import client.commands.handlers.CommandHandlers;
import common.network.CommandMeta;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Routes commands to appropriate handlers.
 */
public class CommandDispatcher {
    private final CommandExecutionContext context;
    private final List<ClientCommandHandler> localHandlers;

    public CommandDispatcher(CommandExecutionContext context) {
        this.context = context;
        this.localHandlers = List.of(
                new CommandHandlers.ExitHandler(),
                new CommandHandlers.ExecuteScriptHandler()
        );
    }

    /**
     * Dispatch a command to the appropriate handler.
     */
    public boolean dispatch(String commandName, String argument, String sourceLabel) {
        CommandMeta meta = context.getCommandMeta(commandName);
        if (meta == null) {
            context.printUnknownCommand(commandName, sourceLabel);
            return true;
        }

        if (context.shouldValidateRequiredArgument() && meta.requiresArgument() && argument.isBlank()) {
            context.printUsage(meta);
            return true;
        }

        for (ClientCommandHandler handler : localHandlers) {
            if (handler.supports(commandName)) {
                CommandExecutionResult result = handler.handle(context, commandName, argument, meta, sourceLabel);
                return result == CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
            }
        }

        ClientCommandHandler serverHandler = new CommandHandlers.ServerForwardingHandler();
        CommandExecutionResult result = serverHandler.handle(context, commandName, argument, meta, sourceLabel);
        return result == CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
    }
}

FILE: client\src\main\java\client\commands\CommandExecutionContext.java
LINES: 118  BYTES: 5192

package client.commands;

import client.ClientCommandRegistry;
import client.NonBlockingTcpClient;
import client.ui.ClientUI;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandRequest;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.network.AuthCredentials;
import common.network.CommandMeta;
import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;

public class CommandExecutionContext {
    private static final int SEND_TIMEOUT_MILLIS = 15000;
    private static final int RETRY_DELAY_MILLIS = 500;

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface BandReader { MusicBand read() throws Exception; }

    @FunctionalInterface
    public interface ScriptRunner { boolean execute(String fileName); }

    private final ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry;
    private final NonBlockingTcpClient tcpClient;
    private final BandReader bandReader;
    private final ScriptRunner scriptRunner;
    private final boolean validateRequiredArgument;
    private final boolean scriptMode;
    private final int maxRetries;
    private final ClientUI ui;
    private AuthCredentials credentials;

    public CommandExecutionContext(ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry,
                                   NonBlockingTcpClient tcpClient,
                                   BandReader bandReader,
                                   ScriptRunner scriptRunner,
                                   boolean validateRequiredArgument,
                                   boolean scriptMode,
                                   int maxRetries,
                                   ClientUI ui) {
        this.commandRegistry = commandRegistry;
        this.tcpClient = tcpClient;
        this.bandReader = bandReader;
        this.scriptRunner = scriptRunner;
        this.validateRequiredArgument = validateRequiredArgument;
        this.scriptMode = scriptMode;
        this.maxRetries = Math.max(1, maxRetries);
        this.ui = ui;
    }

    public CommandMeta getCommandMeta(String commandName) { return commandRegistry.get(commandName); }
    public boolean shouldValidateRequiredArgument() { return validateRequiredArgument; }

    public void printUnknownCommand(String commandName, String sourceLabel) {
        if (scriptMode) ui.println("[" + sourceLabel + "] Unknown command: " + commandName);
        else ui.println("Unknown command. Type 'help'.");
    }

    public void printUsage(CommandMeta meta) { ui.println("Usage: " + meta.usage()); }

    public void printInvalidBandData(String sourceLabel, String details) {
        if (scriptMode) ui.println("[" + sourceLabel + "] Invalid band data: " + details);
        else ui.println("Invalid band data: " + details);
    }

    public CommandExecutionResult handleExit(String sourceLabel) {
        if (scriptMode) ui.println("[" + sourceLabel + "] Exit requested.");
        return CommandExecutionResult.STOP;
    }

    public boolean executeScript(String fileName) { return scriptRunner.execute(fileName); }
    public MusicBand readBand() throws Exception { return bandReader.read(); }

    public void forwardToServer(String commandName, String argument, MusicBand band) {
        CommandRequest request = new CommandRequest(argument, band, credentials);
        CommandPacket packet = new CommandPacket(commandName, request);
        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
            try {
                ResponsePacket response = tcpClient.send(packet, SEND_TIMEOUT_MILLIS);
                ui.println(response.getResult().getMessage());
                if (response.getResult().getData() != null) {
                    ui.println(response.getResult().getData().toString());
                }
                return;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                handleSendFailure(commandName, attempt, e);
            }
        }
    }

    private void handleSendFailure(String commandName, int attempt, Exception error) {
        boolean hasRetriesLeft = attempt < maxRetries;
        if (hasRetriesLeft && !scriptMode) {
            ui.println("Server temporarily unavailable, retrying...");
            try { Thread.sleep(RETRY_DELAY_MILLIS); }
            catch (InterruptedException e) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); }
            return;
        }
        if (scriptMode) {
            ui.println("Server unavailable while executing script command '" + commandName + "': " + error.getMessage());
        } else {
            ui.println("Failed to reach server: " + error.getMessage());
        }
    }

    public void setCredentials(AuthCredentials credentials) { this.credentials = credentials; }

    public boolean hasCredentials() {
        return credentials != null
                && credentials.getLogin() != null && !credentials.getLogin().isBlank()
                && credentials.getPasswordHash() != null && !credentials.getPasswordHash().isBlank();
    }

    public void printAuthRequired(String sourceLabel) {
        if (scriptMode) ui.println("[" + sourceLabel + "] Authentication required.");
        else ui.println("Authentication required.");
    }
}

public enum CommandExecutionResult {

}

package client.commands.handlers;

import client.commands.ClientCommandHandler;
import client.commands.CommandExecutionContext;
import client.commands.CommandExecutionResult;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.network.CommandMeta;

/**
 * All command handlers in one place.
 */
public final class CommandHandlers {

    public static class ExitHandler implements ClientCommandHandler {
        @Override
        public boolean supports(String commandName) {
            return "exit".equals(commandName);
        }

        @Override
        public CommandExecutionResult handle(CommandExecutionContext context,
                                             String commandName,
                                             String argument,
                                             CommandMeta meta,
                                             String sourceLabel) {
            return context.handleExit(sourceLabel);
        }
    }

    public static class ExecuteScriptHandler implements ClientCommandHandler {
        @Override
        public boolean supports(String commandName) {
            return "execute_script".equals(commandName);
        }

        @Override
        public CommandExecutionResult handle(CommandExecutionContext context,
                                             String commandName,
                                             String argument,
                                             CommandMeta meta,
                                             String sourceLabel) {
            if (argument.isBlank()) {
                context.printUsage(meta);
                return CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
            }
            boolean success = context.executeScript(argument);
            return success ? CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE : CommandExecutionResult.STOP;
        }
    }

    public static class ServerForwardingHandler implements ClientCommandHandler {
        @Override
        public boolean supports(String commandName) {
            return true;
        }

        @Override
        public CommandExecutionResult handle(CommandExecutionContext context,
                                             String commandName,
                                             String argument,
                                             CommandMeta meta,
                                             String sourceLabel) {
            if (!context.hasCredentials()) {
                context.printAuthRequired(sourceLabel);
                return CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
            }

            MusicBand band = null;

            if (meta.requiresBand()) {
                try {
                    band = context.readBand();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    context.printInvalidBandData(sourceLabel, e.getMessage());
                    return CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
                }
            }

            context.forwardToServer(commandName, argument, band);
            return CommandExecutionResult.CONTINUE;
        }
    }

}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import common.models.Coordinates;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.models.MusicGenre;
import common.models.Studio;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;

public class BandEditDialog extends Dialog<MusicBand> {

        );

                getDialogPane().getButtonTypes().get(0)).setVisible(false);

                getDialogPane().getButtonTypes().get(1)).setVisible(false);

        content.getStyleClass().add("dialog-content");
        content.setPadding(new Insets(16));

        title.getStyleClass().add("dialog-title");

        closeBtn.setOnAction(e -> hide());
        HBox spacer = new HBox();

        header.getChildren().addAll(title, spacer, closeBtn);
        getDialogPane().setHeader(null);

        GridPane form = new GridPane();
        form.setHgap(12);
        form.setVgap(10);
        form.setPadding(new Insets(0));

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_NAME)), 0, 0);
        form.add(nameField, 0, 1);
        GridPane.setHgrow(nameField, Priority.ALWAYS);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_X) + " ( > -431)"), 0, 2);
        form.add(xField, 0, 3);

        form.add(yField, 0, 5);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_PARTICIPANTS) + " ( > 0)"), 0, 6);
        form.add(participantsField, 0, 7);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_SINGLES) + " ( > 0)"), 0, 8);
        form.add(singlesField, 0, 9);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_DATE)), 0, 10);
        form.add(datePicker, 0, 11);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_GENRE)), 0, 12);
        form.add(genreCombo, 0, 13);

        form.add(labelFor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_STUDIO)), 0, 14);
        form.add(studioField, 0, 15);

        errorLabel.getStyleClass().add("form-error");
        errorLabel.setWrapText(true);

        HBox footer = new HBox(8);
        footer.getStyleClass().add("dialog-footer");
        footer.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
        footer.getChildren().addAll(cancelButton, saveButton);

        content.getChildren().addAll(header, form, errorLabel, footer);

        styleField(nameField);
        styleField(xField);
        styleField(yField);
        styleField(participantsField);
        styleField(singlesField);
        styleField(studioField);
        datePicker.getStyleClass().add("form-field");
        datePicker.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        genreCombo.getStyleClass().add("form-field");
        genreCombo.setItems(FXCollections.observableArrayList(MusicGenre.values()));
        genreCombo.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);

        getDialogPane().setContent(content);
        getDialogPane().setExpandableContent(null);

        if (existing != null) {
            nameField.setText(existing.getName());
            xField.setText(formatDecimal(existing.getCoordinates().getX()));
            yField.setText(String.valueOf(existing.getCoordinates().getY()));
            participantsField.setText(String.valueOf(existing.getNumberOfParticipants()));
            singlesField.setText(existing.getSinglesCount() == null ? "" : String.valueOf(existing.getSinglesCount()));
            datePicker.setValue(existing.getEstablishmentDate());
            genreCombo.setValue(existing.getGenre());
            studioField.setText(existing.getStudio() == null ? "" : existing.getStudio().getName());
        } else {
            xField.setText("0");
            yField.setText("0");
            participantsField.setText("1");
            singlesField.setText("1");
            genreCombo.setValue(MusicGenre.POST_ROCK);
        }

        cancelButton.setOnAction(e -> {
            hide();
            setResult(null);
        });
        saveButton.setOnAction(e -> {
            MusicBand result = tryBuild();
            if (result != null) {
                setResult(result);
                hide();
            }
        });

        setResultConverter(dialogButton -> {
            if (dialogButton == null) return null;
            if (dialogButton.getButtonData() == javafx.scene.control.ButtonBar.ButtonData.OK_DONE) {
                return tryBuild();
            }
            return null;
        });
    }

    private Label labelFor(String text) {
        Label lbl = new Label(text);
        lbl.getStyleClass().add("form-label");
        return lbl;
    }

    private void styleField(TextField field) {
        field.getStyleClass().add("form-field");
        field.setMaxWidth(Double.MAX_VALUE);
        field.focusedProperty().addListener((obs, wasFocused, isNowFocused) -> {
            if (isNowFocused) {
                Platform.runLater(field::selectAll);
            }
        });
    }

    private String formatDecimal(double value) {
        return UiText.decimalFormat(appState.getLocale(), 0, 6).format(value);
    }

    private MusicBand tryBuild() {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        try {
            String name = nameField.getText() == null ? "" : nameField.getText().trim();
            if (name.isEmpty()) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_NAME_REQUIRED));
                return null;
            }

            double x;
            try {
                x = Double.parseDouble(xField.getText().trim().replace(',', '.'));
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_X_NOT_NUMBER));
                return null;
            }
            if (x <= -431) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_X_RANGE));
                return null;
            }

            Integer y;
            try {
                y = Integer.parseInt(yField.getText().trim());
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_Y_NOT_INTEGER));
                return null;
            }

            long participants;
            try {
                participants = Long.parseLong(participantsField.getText().trim());
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_PARTICIPANTS_NOT_NUMBER));
                return null;
            }
            if (participants <= 0) {
                errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_PARTICIPANTS_RANGE));
                return null;
            }

            Integer singles = null;
            String singlesText = singlesField.getText() == null ? "" : singlesField.getText().trim();
            if (!singlesText.isEmpty()) {
                try {
                    singles = Integer.parseInt(singlesText);
                } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                    errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_SINGLES_NOT_NUMBER));
                    return null;
                }
                if (singles <= 0) {
                    errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_SINGLES_RANGE));
                    return null;
                }
            }

            LocalDate establishmentDate = datePicker.getValue();
            MusicGenre genre = genreCombo.getValue();
            String studioName = studioField.getText() == null ? "" : studioField.getText().trim();

            MusicBand band = new MusicBand(
                    name,
                    new Coordinates(x, y),
                    participants,
                    singles,
                    establishmentDate,
                    genre,
                    studioName.isEmpty() ? null : new Studio(studioName)
            );
            errorLabel.setText("");
            return band;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            errorLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VALIDATION_GENERIC_ERROR) + e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }
}

public class BandTableView extends TableView<MusicBand> {

    private final AppState appState;
    private final BandColorManager colorManager;
    private final TextField filterField;
    private final FilteredList<MusicBand> filteredBands;
    private final SortedList<MusicBand> sortedBands;

    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> idCol;
    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> nameCol;
    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> ownerCol;
    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> xCol;
    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> yCol;

    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> singlesCol;

    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> establishmentCol;
    private TableColumn<MusicBand, ?> creationCol;

    public BandTableView(AppState appState, TextField filterField) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.colorManager = new BandColorManager(appState);
        this.filterField = filterField;

        getStyleClass().add("band-table");
        setColumnResizePolicy(TableView.CONSTRAINED_RESIZE_POLICY);

        ObservableList<MusicBand> backingList = appState.getItems();
        this.filteredBands = new FilteredList<>(backingList, band -> applyFilterQuery(band, currentQuery()));
        this.sortedBands = new SortedList<>(filteredBands);
        sortedBands.comparatorProperty().bind(comparatorProperty());
        setItems(sortedBands);

        buildColumns();

        if (filterField != null) {
            filterField.textProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
                String q = currentQuery();
                Predicate<MusicBand> predicate = band -> applyFilterQuery(band, q);
                filteredBands.setPredicate(predicate);
                updateEmptyState();
            });
        }

        backingList.addListener((ListChangeListener<MusicBand>) c -> updateEmptyState());
        attachContextMenu();
        updateEmptyState();

        appState.localeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(this::applyLocale));
    }

    private String currentQuery() {
        if (filterField == null) return "";
        String t = filterField.getText();
        return t == null ? "" : t.toLowerCase().trim();
    }

    /** Filter a single band using the Streams-API logic used by applyStreamsFilter. */
    private boolean applyFilterQuery(MusicBand band, String qLower) {
        if (qLower == null || qLower.isEmpty()) return true;
        if (band == null) return false;
        return filterStream(Stream.of(band), qLower).findAny().isPresent();
    }

    /**
     * Build a filtered+sorted view of the current items using the Streams API.
     * Required by the lab spec: "Ð¡Ð¾Ñ€Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²ÐºÑƒ Ð¸ Ñ„Ð¸Ð»ÑŒÑ‚Ñ€Ð°Ñ†Ð¸ÑŽ Ð·Ð½Ð°Ñ‡ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ð¹ ÑÑ‚Ð¾Ð»Ð±Ñ†Ð¾Ð²
     * Ñ€ÐµÐ°Ð»Ð¸Ð·Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ñ‚ÑŒ Ñ Ð¿Ð¾Ð¼Ð¾Ñ‰ÑŒÑŽ Streams API."
     */
    public ObservableList<MusicBand> applyStreamsFilter() {
        return applyStreamsFilter(currentQuery(), getComparator());
    }

    public ObservableList<MusicBand> applyStreamsFilter(String queryLower, Comparator<MusicBand> comparator) {
        Stream<MusicBand> stream = appState.getItems().stream().filter(Objects::nonNull);
        stream = filterStream(stream, queryLower);
        if (comparator != null) {
            stream = stream.sorted(comparator);
        }
        return FXCollections.observableArrayList(stream.collect(Collectors.toList()));
    }

    private Stream<MusicBand> filterStream(Stream<MusicBand> stream, String qLower) {
        if (qLower == null || qLower.isEmpty()) return stream;
        return stream.filter(band -> {
            if (containsCI(band.getName(), qLower)) return true;
            if (containsCI(band.getOwnerLogin(), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getGenre() != null && containsCI(band.getGenre().name(), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getStudio() != null && containsCI(band.getStudio().getName(), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getCoordinates() != null) {
                if (containsCI(formatCoordValue(band.getCoordinates().getX()), qLower)) return true;
                if (containsCI(String.valueOf(band.getCoordinates().getY()), qLower)) return true;
            }
            if (band.getNumberOfParticipants() != null
                    && containsCI(String.valueOf(band.getNumberOfParticipants()), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getSinglesCount() != null
                    && containsCI(String.valueOf(band.getSinglesCount()), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getEstablishmentDate() != null
                    && containsCI(band.getEstablishmentDate().toString(), qLower)) return true;
            if (band.getCreationDate() != null
                    && containsCI(band.getCreationDate().toString(), qLower)) return true;
            return false;
        });
    }

    private String formatCoordValue(double x) {
        return UiText.decimalFormat(appState.getLocale(), 1, 1).format(x);
    }

    public void applyLocale() {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        if (idCol != null)            idCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_ID));
        if (nameCol != null)          nameCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_NAME));
        if (ownerCol != null)         ownerCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_OWNER));
        if (xCol != null)             xCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_X));
        if (yCol != null)             yCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_Y));
        if (participantsCol != null)  participantsCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_PARTICIPANTS));
        if (singlesCol != null)       singlesCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_SINGLES));
        if (genreCol != null)         genreCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_GENRE));
        if (studioCol != null)        studioCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_STUDIO));
        if (establishmentCol != null) establishmentCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_DATE));
        if (creationCol != null)      creationCol.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_CREATION));
        updateEmptyState();
    }

        TableColumn<MusicBand, Void> actionsCol = new TableColumn<>();
        actionsCol.setCellFactory(col -> new TableCell<>() {
            private final Button editBtn = new Button("âœŽ");
            private final Button deleteBtn = new Button("ðŸ—‘");
            private final HBox box = new HBox(4, editBtn, deleteBtn);

            {
                editBtn.getStyleClass().addAll("action-button");
                deleteBtn.getStyleClass().addAll("action-button", "delete-action");
                box.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
                editBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
                    MusicBand band = getTableRow() == null ? null : getTableRow().getItem();
                    if (band != null) fireEvent(new BandEditEvent(band));
                });
                deleteBtn.setOnAction(e -> {
                    MusicBand band = getTableRow() == null ? null : getTableRow().getItem();
                    if (band != null) fireEvent(new BandDeleteEvent(band));
                });
            }

            @Override
            protected void updateItem(Void item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                if (empty || getTableRow() == null || getTableRow().getItem() == null) {
                    setText(null);
                    setGraphic(null);
                } else {
                    MusicBand band = getTableRow().getItem();
                    String currentUser = appState.getCurrentUser();
                    boolean isOwner = band.getOwnerLogin() != null && band.getOwnerLogin().equals(currentUser);
                    editBtn.setVisible(isOwner);
                    editBtn.setManaged(isOwner);
                    deleteBtn.setVisible(isOwner);
                    deleteBtn.setManaged(isOwner);
                    if (isOwner) {
                        getStyleClass().add("actions-cell");
                        setGraphic(box);
                    } else {
                        setGraphic(null);
                    }
                    setText(null);
                }
            }
        });
        actionsCol.setPrefWidth(100);
        actionsCol.setSortable(false);

        getColumns().addAll(idCol, colorCol, nameCol, ownerCol, xCol, yCol, participantsCol, singlesCol, genreCol, studioCol, establishmentCol, creationCol, actionsCol);
    }

    private void updateEmptyState() {
        if (getItems().isEmpty() && !appState.getItems().isEmpty()) {
            setPlaceholder(emptyStateLabel());
        } else if (appState.getItems().isEmpty()) {
            setPlaceholder(emptyStateLabel());
        }
    }

    private Label emptyStateLabel() {
        Label label = new Label(UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.NO_BANDS));
        label.getStyleClass().add("empty-state");
        return label;
    }

    public void applyTheme(String theme) {
        getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        getStyleClass().add("theme-" + theme);
    }

    public void refresh() {
        updateEmptyState();
    }

    public MusicBand getSelectedBand() {
        return getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
    }

    private String formatInteger(Number value) {
        if (value == null) return "â€”";
        return UiText.integerFormat(appState.getLocale()).format(value);
    }

    private String formatDecimal(Number value, int fractionDigits) {
        if (value == null) return "â€”";
        return UiText.decimalFormat(appState.getLocale(), fractionDigits, fractionDigits).format(value);
    }

    public static class BandEditEvent extends javafx.event.Event {
        public static final javafx.event.EventType<BandEditEvent> EDIT = new javafx.event.EventType<>(javafx.event.Event.ANY, "BAND_EDIT");
        private final MusicBand band;

        public BandEditEvent(MusicBand band) {
            super(EDIT);
            this.band = band;
        }

        public MusicBand getBand() {
            return band;
        }
    }

    public static class BandDeleteEvent extends javafx.event.Event {
        public static final javafx.event.EventType<BandDeleteEvent> DELETE = new javafx.event.EventType<>(javafx.event.Event.ANY, "BAND_DELETE");
        private final MusicBand band;

        public BandDeleteEvent(MusicBand band) {
            super(DELETE);
            this.band = band;
        }

        public MusicBand getBand() {
            return band;
        }
    }

    public static class BandRemoveGreaterEvent extends javafx.event.Event {
        public static final javafx.event.EventType<BandRemoveGreaterEvent> REMOVE_GREATER =
                new javafx.event.EventType<>(javafx.event.Event.ANY, "BAND_REMOVE_GREATER");
        private final MusicBand band;
        public BandRemoveGreaterEvent(MusicBand band) { super(REMOVE_GREATER); this.band = band; }
        public MusicBand getBand() { return band; }
    }

    public static class BandRemoveLowerEvent extends javafx.event.Event {
        public static final javafx.event.EventType<BandRemoveLowerEvent> REMOVE_LOWER =
                new javafx.event.EventType<>(javafx.event.Event.ANY, "BAND_REMOVE_LOWER");
        private final MusicBand band;
        public BandRemoveLowerEvent(MusicBand band) { super(REMOVE_LOWER); this.band = band; }
        public MusicBand getBand() { return band; }
    }

    private void attachContextMenu() {
        setRowFactory(tv -> {
            javafx.scene.control.TableRow<MusicBand> row = new javafx.scene.control.TableRow<>() {
                @Override
                protected void updateItem(MusicBand item, boolean empty) {
                    super.updateItem(item, empty);
                    if (empty || item == null) {
                        setContextMenu(null);
                    }
                }
            };
            row.setOnContextMenuRequested(e -> {
                MusicBand band = row.getItem();
                if (band == null) return;
                String currentUser = appState.getCurrentUser();
                boolean isOwner = band.getOwnerLogin() != null && band.getOwnerLogin().equals(currentUser);
                Locale locale = appState.getLocale();

                javafx.scene.control.ContextMenu menu = new javafx.scene.control.ContextMenu();

                javafx.scene.control.MenuItem editItem = new javafx.scene.control.MenuItem(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.EDIT));
                editItem.setDisable(!isOwner);
                editItem.setOnAction(ev -> fireEvent(new BandEditEvent(band)));

                javafx.scene.control.MenuItem deleteItem = new javafx.scene.control.MenuItem(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.DELETE));
                deleteItem.setDisable(!isOwner);
                deleteItem.setOnAction(ev -> fireEvent(new BandDeleteEvent(band)));

                javafx.scene.control.MenuItem removeGreaterItem = new javafx.scene.control.MenuItem(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_GREATER));
                removeGreaterItem.setDisable(!isOwner);
                removeGreaterItem.setOnAction(ev -> fireEvent(new BandRemoveGreaterEvent(band)));

                javafx.scene.control.MenuItem removeLowerItem = new javafx.scene.control.MenuItem(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_LOWER));
                removeLowerItem.setDisable(!isOwner);
                removeLowerItem.setOnAction(ev -> fireEvent(new BandRemoveLowerEvent(band)));

                menu.getItems().addAll(editItem, deleteItem,
                        new javafx.scene.control.SeparatorMenuItem(),
                        removeGreaterItem, removeLowerItem);
                menu.show(row, e.getScreenX(), e.getScreenY());
            });
            return row;
        });
    }

    private static boolean containsCI(String haystack, String needleLower) {
        return haystack != null && haystack.toLowerCase().contains(needleLower);
    }
}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.javafx.DialogThemer;
import client.javafx.network.BandService;
import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.FXCollections;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonType;
import javafx.scene.control.Dialog;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ListCell;
import javafx.scene.control.ListView;
import javafx.scene.control.TextInputDialog;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Modal command palette exposing every server command to the user.
 * Selecting a command either runs it directly (if it takes no input)
 * or first asks for the required argument.
 */
public class CommandPalette extends Dialog<Void> {

    private record Entry(String commandName, UiText.Key labelKey) {}

    private static final List<Entry> ENTRIES = List.of(
            new Entry("help",                                  UiText.Key.COMMAND_HELP),
            new Entry("info",                                  UiText.Key.COMMAND_INFO),
            new Entry("show",                                  UiText.Key.COMMAND_SHOW),
            new Entry("clear",                                 UiText.Key.COMMAND_CLEAR),
            new Entry("remove_key",                            UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_KEY),
            new Entry("remove_greater_key",                    UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_GREATER_KEY),
            new Entry("count_by_number_of_participants",       UiText.Key.COMMAND_COUNT_BY_PARTICIPANTS),
            new Entry("group_counting_by_name",                UiText.Key.COMMAND_GROUP_BY_NAME),
            new Entry("print_field_descending_singles_count",  UiText.Key.COMMAND_PRINT_SINGLES_DESC)
    );

    private final AppState appState;
    private final BandService service;
    private final ListView<Entry> list;
    private final Consumer<CommandResult> onResult;

    public CommandPalette(AppState appState, BandService service, Consumer<CommandResult> onResult) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.service = service;
        this.onResult = onResult;

        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        getDialogPane().getStyleClass().add("dialog-pane");
        getDialogPane().setPrefSize(520, 460);

        getDialogPane().getButtonTypes().add(ButtonType.CLOSE);

        list = new ListView<>();
        ObservableList<Entry> items = FXCollections.observableArrayList(ENTRIES);
        list.setItems(items);
        list.setCellFactory(param -> new ListCell<>() {
            @Override
            protected void updateItem(Entry item, boolean empty) {
                super.updateItem(item, empty);
                if (empty || item == null) {
                    setText(null);
                    setGraphic(null);
                } else {
                    setText(UiText.text(locale, item.labelKey));
                    getStyleClass().setAll("command-palette-row");
                }
            }
        });
        list.setOnMouseClicked(e -> {
            if (e.getClickCount() == 2) {
                Entry selected = list.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
                if (selected != null) {
                    runEntry(selected);
                }
            }
        });

        VBox content = new VBox(8);
        content.setPadding(new Insets(16));
        Label header = new Label(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        header.getStyleClass().add("dialog-title");

        Button runButton = new Button(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.RUN));
        runButton.getStyleClass().add("dialog-button-save");
        runButton.setOnAction(e -> {
            Entry selected = list.getSelectionModel().getSelectedItem();
            if (selected != null) {
                runEntry(selected);
            }
        });

        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
        HBox footer = new HBox(8, spacer, runButton);
        footer.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);

        content.getChildren().addAll(header, list, footer);
        VBox.setVgrow(list, Priority.ALWAYS);
        getDialogPane().setContent(content);

        list.getSelectionModel().selectFirst();
    }

    private void runEntry(Entry entry) {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        switch (entry.commandName) {
            case "help" -> dispatch(service.help());
            case "info" -> dispatch(service.info());
            case "show" -> dispatch(service.show());
            case "clear" -> confirmAndRun(locale, () -> dispatch(service.clear()));
            case "remove_key" -> promptAndRun(locale, UiText.Key.KEY_LABEL, key -> dispatch(service.removeKey(key)));
            case "remove_greater_key" -> promptAndRun(locale, UiText.Key.KEY_LABEL, key -> dispatch(service.removeGreaterKey(key)));
            case "count_by_number_of_participants" -> promptAndRun(locale, UiText.Key.PARTICIPANTS, value -> {
                try {
                    long n = Long.parseLong(value);
                    dispatch(service.countByNumberOfParticipants(n));
                } catch (NumberFormatException nfe) {
                    setStatus(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + "not a number", "error");
                }
            });
            case "group_counting_by_name" -> dispatch(service.groupCountingByName());
            case "print_field_descending_singles_count" -> dispatch(service.printFieldDescendingSinglesCount());
        }
    }

    private void confirmAndRun(Locale locale, Runnable action) {
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
                UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONFIRM_DELETE_MESSAGE),
                ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        alert.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONFIRM_DELETE_TITLE));
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(alert, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        alert.showAndWait().ifPresent(bt -> {
            if (bt == ButtonType.YES) action.run();
        });
    }

    private void promptAndRun(Locale locale, UiText.Key labelKey, Consumer<String> action) {
        TextInputDialog dialog = new TextInputDialog();
        dialog.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        dialog.setHeaderText(UiText.text(locale, labelKey));
        dialog.setContentText(UiText.text(locale, labelKey) + ":");
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(dialog, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        dialog.showAndWait().ifPresent(action);
    }

    private void dispatch(java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture<CommandResult> future) {
        future.whenComplete((result, ex) -> Platform.runLater(() -> {
            if (ex != null) {
                setStatus(UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + ex.getMessage(), "error");
                return;
            }
            if (result == null) {
                setStatus(UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + "no result", "error");
                return;
            }
            if (!result.isSuccess()) {
                setStatus(UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + result.getMessage(), "error");
            } else {
                setStatus(UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.OPERATION_SUCCESS) + " â€” " + result.getMessage(), "success");
            }
            if (onResult != null) onResult.accept(result);
            hide();
        }));
    }

    private void setStatus(String message, String kind) {
        appState.setStatus(message, kind);
    }
}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import common.network.CommandMeta;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Dialog;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.control.ScrollPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.text.Font;
import javafx.scene.text.FontWeight;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * About / Help dialog. Lists every public command with its description
 * and usage, plus a small "About" section.
 */
public class HelpDialog extends Dialog<Void> {

    public HelpDialog(AppState appState, Map<String, CommandMeta> commands) {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();

        setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.HELP_TITLE));
        getDialogPane().getStyleClass().add("dialog-pane");
        getDialogPane().setPrefWidth(560);
        getDialogPane().setPrefHeight(560);

        VBox content = new VBox(16);
        content.setPadding(new Insets(24, 24, 16, 24));
        content.getStyleClass().add("dialog-content");

        Label title = new Label(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.HELP_TITLE));
        title.getStyleClass().add("dialog-title");
        title.setFont(Font.font("Inter", FontWeight.SEMI_BOLD, 18));
        content.getChildren().add(title);

        Label subtitle = new Label(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.HELP_SUBTITLE));
        subtitle.getStyleClass().add("field-label");
        subtitle.setWrapText(true);
        content.getChildren().add(subtitle);

        ScrollPane scroll = new ScrollPane();
        scroll.setFitToWidth(true);
        scroll.setPrefHeight(320);
        scroll.getStyleClass().add("help-scroll");
        scroll.setStyle("-fx-background-color: transparent; -fx-background: transparent;");

        VBox commandsList = new VBox(6);
        for (Map.Entry<String, CommandMeta> entry : commands.entrySet()) {
            String name = entry.getKey();
            String description = entry.getValue() == null ? "" : safeString(entry.getValue().description());
            String usage = entry.getValue() == null ? name : safeString(entry.getValue().usage());
            if (name == null || name.startsWith("__")) continue;
            commandsList.getChildren().add(buildCommandRow(name, description, usage));
        }
        scroll.setContent(commandsList);

        content.getChildren().add(scroll);

        Label about = new Label(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.HELP_ABOUT));
        about.getStyleClass().add("field-label");
        about.setWrapText(true);
        content.getChildren().add(about);

        getDialogPane().setContent(content);
        getDialogPane().getButtonTypes().add(javafx.scene.control.ButtonType.CLOSE);
    }

    public HelpDialog(AppState appState, List<CommandMeta> commandMetas) {
        this(appState, toMap(commandMetas));
    }

    private static Map<String, CommandMeta> toMap(List<CommandMeta> commandMetas) {
        java.util.Map<String, CommandMeta> map = new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (CommandMeta meta : commandMetas) {
            if (meta == null) continue;
            String name = meta.usage() == null ? "" : meta.usage().split(" ", 2)[0];
            map.put(name, meta);
        }
        return map;
    }

    private Node buildCommandRow(String name, String description, String usage) {
        VBox card = new VBox(4);
        card.getStyleClass().add("help-command-card");

        HBox header = new HBox(8);
        header.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);

        Label nameLabel = new Label(name);
        nameLabel.getStyleClass().add("help-command-name");
        Region spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        Label usageLabel = new Label(usage);
        usageLabel.getStyleClass().add("help-command-usage");

        header.getChildren().addAll(nameLabel, spacer, usageLabel);

        Label descLabel = new Label(description);
        descLabel.getStyleClass().add("help-command-desc");
        descLabel.setWrapText(true);

        card.getChildren().addAll(header, descLabel);
        return card;
    }

    private static String safeString(String s) {
        return s == null ? "" : s;
    }
}

public class SidebarPane extends VBox {

        langGrid = new HBox(4);
        langGrid.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER);
        langGroup = new ToggleGroup();

        for (String lang : LOCALES) {
            ToggleButton btn = new ToggleButton(lang.split("-")[0].toUpperCase());

    public void applyLocale(Locale locale) {
        welcomeLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.WELCOME));
        navDashboardLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.DASHBOARD));
        langHeaderLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.LANGUAGE).toUpperCase());
        logoutLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.LOGOUT));
        updateUsernameLabel(locale);
        syncLangToggle();
    }

    public void applyTheme(String theme) {
        getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        getStyleClass().add("theme-" + theme);
        if (userCard != null) updateUserAvatar();
    }

    private void updateUsernameLabel(Locale locale) {
        String user = appState.getCurrentUser();
        usernameLabel.setText(user != null ? user : "â€”");
    }

    private void updateUserAvatar() {
        String user = appState.getCurrentUser();
        if (user == null || user.isBlank()) {
            userAvatar.setStyle("-fx-fill: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.2); -fx-stroke: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.4);");
        } else {
            String color = colorManager.getColorForUser(user);
            userAvatar.setStyle("-fx-fill: " + toRGBA(color, 0.2) + "; -fx-stroke: " + toRGBA(color, 0.4) + ";");
        }
    }

    private static String toRGBA(String hexColor, double alpha) {
        if (hexColor == null || !hexColor.startsWith("#") || hexColor.length() != 7) {
            return "rgba(148, 163, 184, " + alpha + ")";
        }
        try {
            int r = Integer.parseInt(hexColor.substring(1, 3), 16);
            int g = Integer.parseInt(hexColor.substring(3, 5), 16);
            int b = Integer.parseInt(hexColor.substring(5, 7), 16);
            return String.format("rgba(%d, %d, %d, %.2f)", r, g, b, alpha);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return "rgba(148, 163, 184, " + alpha + ")";
        }
    }

    private void syncLangToggle() {
        String current = UiText.codeFromLocale(appState.getLocale());
        for (javafx.scene.Node node : langGrid.getChildren()) {
            if (node instanceof ToggleButton btn) {
                if (current.equals(btn.getUserData())) {
                    btn.setSelected(true);
                } else {
                    btn.setSelected(false);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static Locale localeFromCode(String code) {
        return UiText.localeFromCode(code);
    }

    public static String codeFromLocale(Locale locale) {
        return UiText.codeFromLocale(locale);
    }

    public Button getLogoutButton() {
        return logoutButton;
    }
}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.ui.AppState;
import javafx.scene.control.ToggleButton;
import javafx.scene.control.ToggleGroup;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;

public class ThemeTogglePane extends HBox {

    private final AppState appState;
    private final ToggleGroup themeGroup;
    private final ToggleButton lightButton;
    private final ToggleButton darkButton;

    public ThemeTogglePane(AppState appState) {
        this.appState = appState;
        getStyleClass().add("theme-toggle-pane");
        setAlignment(javafx.geometry.Pos.CENTER);

        themeGroup = new ToggleGroup();

        lightButton = new ToggleButton("â˜€");
        lightButton.getStyleClass().add("theme-toggle-button");
        lightButton.setToggleGroup(themeGroup);
        lightButton.setUserData("light");
        lightButton.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (lightButton.isSelected()) {
                appState.setTheme("light");
            }
        });

        darkButton = new ToggleButton("ðŸŒ™");
        darkButton.getStyleClass().add("theme-toggle-button");
        darkButton.setToggleGroup(themeGroup);
        darkButton.setUserData("dark");
        darkButton.setOnAction(e -> {
            if (darkButton.isSelected()) {
                appState.setTheme("dark");
            }
        });

        getChildren().addAll(lightButton, darkButton);

        syncToggle(appState.getTheme());

        appState.themeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> syncToggle(newVal)));
    }

    private void syncToggle(String theme) {
        switch (theme) {
            case "dark" -> darkButton.setSelected(true);
            default -> lightButton.setSelected(true);
        }
    }
}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import javafx.animation.Transition;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.StackPane;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.util.Duration;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Floating toast stack for the main view.  Add via {@link #show(String, Kind)}
 * or one of the kind-specific helpers.  Toasts auto-dismiss after
 * {@link #TOAST_LIFETIME_MILLIS} and can be clicked to dismiss early.
 * Stacks at the bottom-right of its parent.
 */
public class ToastPane extends VBox {

    public enum Kind { INFO, SUCCESS, WARNING, ERROR }

    public static final long TOAST_LIFETIME_MILLIS = 4000L;

    public ToastPane() {
        setSpacing(8);
        setAlignment(Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
        setPickOnBounds(false);
        StackPane.setAlignment(this, Pos.BOTTOM_RIGHT);
        StackPane.setMargin(this, new Insets(0, 20, 20, 0));
    }

    public void show(String message, Kind kind) {
        if (message == null || message.isBlank()) return;
        Platform.runLater(() -> {
            Node node = buildToastNode(message, kind == null ? Kind.INFO : kind);
            getChildren().add(node);

            node.setOpacity(0);
            node.setTranslateY(20);
            Transition fadeIn = new Transition() {
                { setCycleDuration(Duration.millis(220)); }
                @Override protected void interpolate(double frac) {
                    node.setOpacity(frac);
                    node.setTranslateY(20 * (1 - frac));
                }
            };
            fadeIn.play();

            new Thread(() -> {
                try { Thread.sleep(TOAST_LIFETIME_MILLIS); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; }
                Platform.runLater(() -> dismiss(node));
            }, "toast-dismiss").start();
        });
    }

    public void info(String message)    { show(message, Kind.INFO); }
    public void success(String message) { show(message, Kind.SUCCESS); }
    public void warn(String message)    { show(message, Kind.WARNING); }
    public void error(String message)   { show(message, Kind.ERROR); }

    public void localized(Locale locale, UiText.Key key, String fallback, Kind kind) {
        String text = UiText.text(locale, key);
        show(text.isEmpty() ? fallback : text, kind);
    }

    private Node buildToastNode(String message, Kind kind) {
        Label label = new Label(message);
        label.setWrapText(true);
        label.getStyleClass().addAll("toast", "toast-" + kind.name().toLowerCase());
        label.setOnMouseClicked(e -> dismiss(label));
        return label;
    }

    private void dismiss(Node node) {
        if (!getChildren().contains(node)) return;
        Transition fadeOut = new Transition() {
            { setCycleDuration(Duration.millis(180)); }
            @Override protected void interpolate(double frac) {
                node.setOpacity(1 - frac);
                node.setTranslateY(20 * frac);
            }
        };
        fadeOut.setOnFinished(e -> getChildren().remove(node));
        fadeOut.play();
    }
}

package client.javafx.components;

import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import javafx.animation.KeyFrame;
import javafx.animation.KeyValue;
import javafx.animation.Timeline;
import javafx.geometry.Insets;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;

import javafx.scene.text.Text;
import javafx.util.Duration;

import java.util.Locale;

public class TopBarPane extends HBox {

    private final AppState appState;

    private final HBox syncBadge;
    private final Circle syncPulse;
    private final Label syncLabel;
    private final Region spacer;
    private final ThemeTogglePane themeToggle;
    private final Button commandsButton;
    private final Text commandsIcon;
    private final Label commandsLabel;
    private final Button addButton;
    private final Text addIcon;
    private final Label addLabel;

    private Timeline pulseAnim;
    private AppState.ConnectionState currentState = AppState.ConnectionState.CONNECTING;

    public TopBarPane(AppState appState) {
        this.appState = appState;
        getStyleClass().add("top-bar-pane");
        setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        setPadding(new Insets(0, 20, 0, 20));

        syncBadge = new HBox(6);
        syncBadge.getStyleClass().add("sync-badge");
        syncBadge.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        syncPulse = new Circle(4);
        syncPulse.getStyleClass().add("sync-pulse");
        syncLabel = new Label();
        syncLabel.getStyleClass().add("sync-label");
        syncBadge.getChildren().addAll(syncPulse, syncLabel);

        spacer = new Region();
        HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);

        themeToggle = new ThemeTogglePane(appState);

        commandsButton = new Button();
        commandsButton.getStyleClass().add("commands-button");
        commandsIcon = new Text("âŒ˜");
        commandsIcon.getStyleClass().add("commands-button-icon");
        commandsLabel = new Label();
        commandsLabel.getStyleClass().add("commands-button-label");
        HBox commandsContent = new HBox(6, commandsIcon, commandsLabel);
        commandsContent.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        commandsButton.setGraphic(commandsContent);

        addButton = new Button();
        addButton.getStyleClass().add("add-button");
        addIcon = new Text("+");
        addIcon.getStyleClass().add("add-button-icon");
        addLabel = new Label();
        addLabel.getStyleClass().add("add-button-label");
        HBox addContent = new HBox(6, addIcon, addLabel);
        addContent.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        addButton.setGraphic(addContent);

        getChildren().addAll(syncBadge, spacer, themeToggle, commandsButton, addButton);

        applyLocale(appState.getLocale());
        applyTheme(appState.getTheme());

        appState.localeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> applyLocale(newVal)));
        appState.themeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(() -> applyTheme(newVal)));
        appState.connectionStateProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                javafx.application.Platform.runLater(this::updateConnectionLabel));
    }

    public void applyLocale(Locale locale) {
        addLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.ADD));
        commandsLabel.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        updateConnectionLabel();
    }

    public void updateConnectionLabel() {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        AppState.ConnectionState state = appState.getConnectionState();
        String text = switch (state) {
            case CONNECTED    -> UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONN_CONNECTED);
            case CONNECTING   -> UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONN_CONNECTING);
            case DISCONNECTED -> UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONN_DISCONNECTED);
        };
        syncLabel.setText(text);
        syncBadge.getStyleClass().removeAll("connected", "connecting", "disconnected");
        syncBadge.getStyleClass().add(state.name().toLowerCase());
        currentState = state;
        updatePulseAnimation();
    }

    private void updatePulseAnimation() {
        if (pulseAnim != null) {
            pulseAnim.stop();
            pulseAnim = null;
        }
        if (currentState == AppState.ConnectionState.CONNECTED) {
            pulseAnim = new Timeline(
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.ZERO, new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 1.0), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 1.0)),
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.millis(900), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 1.35), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 1.35)),
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.millis(1800), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 1.0), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 1.0))
            );
            pulseAnim.setCycleCount(Timeline.INDEFINITE);
            pulseAnim.setAutoReverse(false);
            pulseAnim.play();
        } else if (currentState == AppState.ConnectionState.CONNECTING) {
            pulseAnim = new Timeline(
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.ZERO, new KeyValue(syncPulse.opacityProperty(), 0.4), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 0.85), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 0.85)),
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.millis(600), new KeyValue(syncPulse.opacityProperty(), 1.0), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 1.15), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 1.15)),
                    new KeyFrame(Duration.millis(1200), new KeyValue(syncPulse.opacityProperty(), 0.4), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleXProperty(), 0.85), new KeyValue(syncPulse.scaleYProperty(), 0.85))
            );
            pulseAnim.setCycleCount(Timeline.INDEFINITE);
            pulseAnim.play();
        } else {
            syncPulse.setScaleX(1);
            syncPulse.setScaleY(1);
            syncPulse.setOpacity(0.6);
        }
    }

    public void applyTheme(String theme) {
        getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        getStyleClass().add("theme-" + theme);
    }

    public Button getAddButton() {
        return addButton;
    }

    public Button getCommandsButton() {
        return commandsButton;
    }
}

FILE: client\src\main\java\client\javafx\components\VisualizationCanvas.java
LINES: 761  BYTES: 29507
