package client.javafx.components;

import client.javafx.model.BandColorManager;
import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import javafx.animation.AnimationTimer;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.geometry.Pos;
import javafx.scene.canvas.Canvas;
import javafx.scene.canvas.GraphicsContext;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.Label;
import javafx.scene.input.MouseEvent;
import javafx.scene.layout.HBox;
import javafx.scene.layout.Priority;
import javafx.scene.layout.Region;
import javafx.scene.layout.VBox;
import javafx.scene.paint.Color;
import javafx.scene.text.Font;
import javafx.scene.text.FontWeight;
import javafx.scene.text.TextAlignment;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

public class VisualizationCanvas extends Canvas {

    private final AppState appState;
    private final BandColorManager colorManager;
    private final VBox detailsPopup;
    private final VBox detailsContent;

    private final Map<Long, RenderState> renderStates = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Long, Long> lastSeenIds = new HashMap<>();
    private AnimationTimer animationTimer;

    private double mouseX = 0;
    private double mouseY = 0;
    private boolean mouseInside = false;
    private long hoveredBandId = -1L;
    private long selectedBandId = -1L;
    private double panelWidth = 0;
    private double panelHeight = 0;
    private double pulsePhase = 0;
    private double scale = 1.0;
    private double panX = 0;
    private double panY = 0;
    private double zoom = 1.0;
    private static final double MIN_ZOOM = 0.05;
    private static final double MAX_ZOOM = 20.0;
    private boolean isPanning = false;
    private double panStartX = 0;
    private double panStartY = 0;
    private double panOriginX = 0;
    private double panOriginY = 0;
    private boolean isDraggingBand = false;

    private final Button editButton = new Button();
    private final Button deleteButton = new Button();
    private MusicBand selectedBand;

    public VisualizationCanvas(AppState appState, VBox detailsPopup, VBox detailsContent) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.colorManager = new BandColorManager(appState);
        this.detailsPopup = detailsPopup;
        this.detailsContent = detailsContent;

        getStyleClass().add("visualization-canvas");

        setOnMouseMoved(this::handleMouseMoved);
        setOnMouseEntered(e -> {
            mouseInside = true;
            setCursor(javafx.scene.Cursor.CROSSHAIR);
        });
        setOnMouseExited(e -> {
            mouseInside = false;
            mouseX = 0;
            mouseY = 0;
            hoveredBandId = -1L;
            setCursor(javafx.scene.Cursor.DEFAULT);
            updateCoordLabel();
        });

        // Drag to pan (left button on empty area, or middle button anywhere).
        // Released clicks on a band still fire setOnMouseClicked below.
        setOnMousePressed(e -> {
            if (e.getButton() == javafx.scene.input.MouseButton.MIDDLE
                    || (e.getButton() == javafx.scene.input.MouseButton.PRIMARY
                        && findBandAt(e.getX(), e.getY()) == -1L)) {
                isPanning = true;
                panStartX = e.getX();
                panStartY = e.getY();
                panOriginX = panX;
                panOriginY = panY;
                setCursor(javafx.scene.Cursor.MOVE);
            }
        });
        setOnMouseDragged(e -> {
            if (isPanning) {
                panX = panOriginX + (e.getX() - panStartX);
                panY = panOriginY + (e.getY() - panStartY);
            }
        });
        setOnMouseReleased(e -> {
            if (isPanning) {
                isPanning = false;
                setCursor(mouseInside
                        ? javafx.scene.Cursor.CROSSHAIR
                        : javafx.scene.Cursor.DEFAULT);
            }
        });

        // Scroll wheel to zoom, centered on the mouse position.
        setOnScroll(e -> {
            double factor = (e.getDeltaY() > 0) ? 1.15 : 1.0 / 1.15;
            double oldZoom = zoom;
            double newZoom = Math.max(MIN_ZOOM, Math.min(MAX_ZOOM, zoom * factor));
            if (newZoom == oldZoom) return;
            // Keep the world point under the mouse fixed on screen.
            double mx = e.getX() - panelWidth / 2.0;
            double my = e.getY() - panelHeight / 2.0;
            double worldXBefore = (mx - panX) / (scale * oldZoom);
            double worldYBefore = (my - panY) / (scale * oldZoom);
            zoom = newZoom;
            panX = mx - worldXBefore * scale * zoom;
            panY = my - worldYBefore * scale * zoom;
        });

        setOnMouseClicked(e -> {
            if (e.getButton() != javafx.scene.input.MouseButton.PRIMARY) return;
            if (e.getClickCount() == 2) {
                panX = 0;
                panY = 0;
                zoom = 1.0;
                return;
            }
            long clickedId = findBandAt(e.getX(), e.getY());
            if (clickedId != -1L) {
                selectedBandId = clickedId;
                selectedBand = findBandById(clickedId);
                showDetailsPopup(clickedId);
            } else {
                selectedBandId = -1L;
                selectedBand = null;
                hideDetailsPopup();
            }
        });

        editButton.getStyleClass().addAll("details-action-button", "edit-action");
        deleteButton.getStyleClass().addAll("details-action-button", "delete-action");
        editButton.setVisible(false);
        editButton.setManaged(false);
        deleteButton.setVisible(false);
        deleteButton.setManaged(false);

        startAnimation();
    }

    /**
     * Sync the canvas's drawing-surface size to the parent's layout size.
     * Capped at {@link #MAX_CANVAS_DIM} defensively.
     */
    public void bindToParentSize(javafx.scene.layout.Region parent) {
        Runnable sync = () -> {
            double w = parent.getWidth();
            double h = parent.getHeight();
            if (!Double.isFinite(w) || w <= 0) return;
            if (!Double.isFinite(h) || h <= 0) return;
            if (w > MAX_CANVAS_DIM) w = MAX_CANVAS_DIM;
            if (h > MAX_CANVAS_DIM) h = MAX_CANVAS_DIM;
            double curW = getWidth();
            double curH = getHeight();
            if (Math.abs(curW - w) > 0.5) setWidth(w);
            if (Math.abs(curH - h) > 0.5) setHeight(h);
        };
        parent.widthProperty().addListener((obs, o, n) -> sync.run());
        parent.heightProperty().addListener((obs, o, n) -> sync.run());
        javafx.application.Platform.runLater(sync);
    }

    public void applyTheme(String theme) {
        getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        getStyleClass().add("theme-" + theme);
    }

    public void applyLocale(Locale locale) {
        editButton.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.EDIT));
        deleteButton.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.DELETE));
    }

    public void refresh() {
    }

    private void startAnimation() {
        animationTimer = new AnimationTimer() {
            @Override
            public void handle(long now) {
                updateAndRender();
            }
        };
        animationTimer.start();
    }

    public void stop() {
        if (animationTimer != null) animationTimer.stop();
    }

    private void handleMouseMoved(MouseEvent event) {
        mouseX = event.getX();
        mouseY = event.getY();
        hoveredBandId = findBandAt(mouseX, mouseY);
        updateCoordLabel();
    }

    private void handleMouseClicked(MouseEvent event) {
        long clickedId = findBandAt(event.getX(), event.getY());
        if (clickedId != -1L) {
            selectedBandId = clickedId;
            selectedBand = findBandById(clickedId);
            showDetailsPopup(clickedId);
        } else {
            selectedBandId = -1L;
            selectedBand = null;
            hideDetailsPopup();
        }
    }

    private long findBandAt(double screenX, double screenY) {
        double w = panelWidth > 0 ? panelWidth : getWidth();
        double h = panelHeight > 0 ? panelHeight : getHeight();
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return -1L;
        double s = Double.isFinite(scale) && scale > 0 ? scale : 1.0;
        double effectiveScale = s * zoom;
        if (effectiveScale <= 0) effectiveScale = 1.0;
        double offsetX = w / 2.0;
        double offsetY = h / 2.0;
        double worldX = (screenX - offsetX - panX) / effectiveScale;
        double worldY = -((screenY - offsetY - panY) / effectiveScale);

        for (MusicBand band : appState.getItems()) {
            if (band == null || band.getCoordinates() == null) continue;
            double r = radiusFor(band);
            double dx = worldX - band.getCoordinates().getX();
            double dy = worldY - band.getCoordinates().getY();
            if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) {
                return band.getId();
            }
        }
        return -1L;
    }

    private double radiusFor(MusicBand band) {
        long p = band.getNumberOfParticipants() == null ? 1 : band.getNumberOfParticipants();
        return Math.max(15, Math.min(60, 10 + p * 3));
    }

    private double scaledRadius(MusicBand band) {
        return Math.max(8.0, radiusFor(band) * scale);
    }

    /** Auto-fit scale so all bands always sit inside the canvas, with padding. */
    private double computeScale() {
        double maxAbs = 1.0;
        int visible = 0;
        for (MusicBand band : appState.getItems()) {
            if (band == null || band.getCoordinates() == null) continue;
            double x = band.getCoordinates().getX();
            double y = band.getCoordinates().getY();
            if (!Double.isFinite(x) || !Double.isFinite(y)) continue;
            double ax = Math.abs(x);
            double ay = Math.abs(y);
            if (ax > maxAbs) maxAbs = ax;
            if (ay > maxAbs) maxAbs = ay;
            visible++;
        }
        if (visible == 0) return 1.0;
        double padding = 60.0;
        double available = Math.min(panelWidth, panelHeight) / 2.0 - padding;
        available -= 30.0;
        if (!Double.isFinite(available) || available <= 0) return 1.0;
        double raw = available / maxAbs;
        if (!Double.isFinite(raw)) return 1.0;
        // Clamp to a sane range so a single band with a huge coordinate
        // doesn't compress every other band to sub-pixel size.
        return Math.max(0.02, Math.min(2.0, raw));
    }

    private void updateCoordLabel() {
    }

    private static final double MAX_CANVAS_DIM = 4000.0;

    private void updateAndRender() {
        double w = getWidth();
        double h = getHeight();
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        if (!Double.isFinite(w) || !Double.isFinite(h)) return;
        if (w > MAX_CANVAS_DIM) w = MAX_CANVAS_DIM;
        if (h > MAX_CANVAS_DIM) h = MAX_CANVAS_DIM;
        panelWidth = w;
        panelHeight = h;

        try {
            doUpdateAndRender();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            System.err.println("[Viz] render error: " + t);
        }
    }

    private void doUpdateAndRender() {
        GraphicsContext gc = getGraphicsContext2D();
        boolean isDark = appState.getTheme().equals("dark");
        scale = computeScale();
        if (!Double.isFinite(scale) || scale <= 0) scale = 1.0;

        gc.clearRect(0, 0, panelWidth, panelHeight);

        drawBackgroundWash(gc, isDark);

        Map<Long, MusicBand> current = new HashMap<>();
        for (MusicBand b : appState.getItems()) {
            if (b != null) current.put(b.getId(), b);
        }
        for (MusicBand b : appState.getItems()) {
            if (b == null || b.getCoordinates() == null) continue;
            long id = b.getId();
            if (!renderStates.containsKey(id)) {
                double sx = b.getCoordinates().getX();
                double sy = b.getCoordinates().getY();
                if (!Double.isFinite(sx)) sx = 0;
                if (!Double.isFinite(sy)) sy = 0;
                renderStates.put(id, new RenderState(sx, sy));
            }
        }
        renderStates.keySet().removeIf(id -> !current.containsKey(id));
        lastSeenIds.keySet().retainAll(current.keySet());

        // pulse the hover ring on each frame
        pulsePhase += 0.06;

        for (MusicBand band : appState.getItems()) {
            if (band == null || band.getCoordinates() == null) continue;
            RenderState state = renderStates.get(band.getId());
            if (state == null) continue;
            double targetX = band.getCoordinates().getX();
            double targetY = band.getCoordinates().getY();
            if (!Double.isFinite(targetX)) targetX = 0;
            if (!Double.isFinite(targetY)) targetY = 0;
            if (!Double.isFinite(state.x))  state.x  = targetX;
            if (!Double.isFinite(state.y))  state.y  = targetY;
            state.scale += (1.0 - state.scale) * 0.18;
            state.opacity += (1.0 - state.opacity) * 0.18;
            state.x += (targetX - state.x) * 0.18;
            state.y += (targetY - state.y) * 0.18;

            drawBand(gc, band, state, isDark);
        }

        if (mouseInside) {
            drawCrosshair(gc, isDark);
            drawCoordTooltip(gc, isDark);
        }

        // empty-state hint
        if (current.isEmpty()) {
            drawEmptyState(gc, isDark);
        }
    }

    private void drawBackgroundWash(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        double cx = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double cy = panelHeight / 2.0;
        double maxR = Math.hypot(Math.max(cx, panelWidth - cx), Math.max(cy, panelHeight - cy));
        javafx.scene.paint.RadialGradient wash = new javafx.scene.paint.RadialGradient(
                0, 0, cx, cy, maxR, false, javafx.scene.paint.CycleMethod.NO_CYCLE,
                new javafx.scene.paint.Stop(0, isDark
                        ? Color.color(0.16, 0.18, 0.22, 0.6)
                        : Color.color(0.95, 0.96, 0.99, 0.6)),
                new javafx.scene.paint.Stop(1, isDark
                        ? Color.color(0.10, 0.10, 0.12, 0)
                        : Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0))
        );
        gc.setFill(wash);
        gc.fillRect(0, 0, panelWidth, panelHeight);
    }

    private void drawEmptyState(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        String text = "No bands yet â€” add one with the + button";
        Font font = Font.font("Inter", FontWeight.MEDIUM, 13);
        gc.setFont(font);
        double tw = textWidth(gc, text);
        double padX = 16, padY = 10;
        double boxW = tw + padX * 2;
        double boxH = 32;
        double x = (panelWidth - boxW) / 2.0;
        double y = (panelHeight - boxH) / 2.0 - 60;
        Color bg = isDark
                ? Color.color(0.10, 0.10, 0.12, 0.70)
                : Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.78);
        Color border = isDark
                ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.08)
                : Color.color(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
        Color fg = isDark
                ? Color.color(0.78, 0.78, 0.84, 1)
                : Color.color(0.45, 0.45, 0.50, 1);
        gc.setFill(bg);
        gc.fillRoundRect(x, y, boxW, boxH, 12, 12);
        gc.setStroke(border);
        gc.setLineWidth(1);
        gc.strokeRoundRect(x + 0.5, y + 0.5, boxW - 1, boxH - 1, 12, 12);
        gc.setFill(fg);
        gc.setTextAlign(TextAlignment.CENTER);
        gc.fillText(text, x + boxW / 2.0, y + padY + 12);
    }

    private void drawAxisLabels(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        double offsetX = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double offsetY = panelHeight / 2.0;
        Color fg = isDark
                ? Color.color(0.55, 0.58, 0.66, 0.9)
                : Color.color(0.40, 0.40, 0.45, 0.85);
        gc.setFill(fg);
        gc.setFont(Font.font("Inter", FontWeight.SEMI_BOLD, 10));
        gc.setTextAlign(TextAlignment.CENTER);
        gc.fillText("X", panelWidth - 12, offsetY - 6);
        gc.setTextAlign(TextAlignment.LEFT);
        gc.fillText("Y", offsetX + 8, 16);
    }

    private void drawGrid(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        gc.setStroke(isDark ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.05) : Color.color(0, 0, 0, 0.05));
        gc.setLineWidth(1);
        double gridSize = 50;
        double offsetX = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double offsetY = panelHeight / 2.0;

        for (double x = offsetX % gridSize; x < panelWidth; x += gridSize) {
            gc.strokeLine(x, 0, x, panelHeight);
        }
        for (double y = offsetY % gridSize; y < panelHeight; y += gridSize) {
            gc.strokeLine(0, y, panelWidth, y);
        }
    }

    private void drawAxes(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        gc.setStroke(isDark ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.2) : Color.color(0, 0, 0, 0.2));
        gc.setLineWidth(2);
        double offsetX = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double offsetY = panelHeight / 2.0;
        gc.strokeLine(offsetX, 0, offsetX, panelHeight);
        gc.strokeLine(0, offsetY, panelWidth, offsetY);
    }

    private void drawCrosshair(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        Color line = isDark
                ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.35)
                : Color.color(0.05, 0.05, 0.1, 0.40);
        gc.setStroke(line);
        gc.setLineWidth(1);
        gc.setLineDashes(4, 4);
        gc.strokeLine(mouseX, 0, mouseX, panelHeight);
        gc.strokeLine(0, mouseY, panelWidth, mouseY);
        gc.setLineDashes(null);

        double dotR = 3.5;
        Color dot = isDark ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.7) : Color.color(0.05, 0.05, 0.1, 0.85);
        gc.setFill(dot);
        gc.fillOval(mouseX - dotR, mouseY - dotR, dotR * 2, dotR * 2);
    }

    private void drawCoordTooltip(GraphicsContext gc, boolean isDark) {
        double offsetX = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double offsetY = panelHeight / 2.0;
        double effectiveScale = scale * zoom;
        if (effectiveScale <= 0 || !Double.isFinite(effectiveScale)) effectiveScale = 1.0;
        double worldX = (mouseX - offsetX - panX) / effectiveScale;
        double worldY = -((mouseY - offsetY - panY) / effectiveScale);
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        String xStr = UiText.decimalFormat(locale, 0, 1).format(worldX);
        String yStr = UiText.decimalFormat(locale, 0, 1).format(worldY);
        String label = "X " + xStr + "  Â·  Y " + yStr;

        Font font = Font.font("Inter", FontWeight.MEDIUM, 11);
        gc.setFont(font);
        double padX = 10, padY = 6;
        double labelW = textWidth(gc, label);
        double boxW = labelW + padX * 2;
        double boxH = 22;

        double margin = 14;
        double boxX = mouseX + margin;
        double boxY = mouseY + margin;
        if (boxX + boxW > panelWidth - 4)  boxX = mouseX - margin - boxW;
        if (boxY + boxH > panelHeight - 4) boxY = mouseY - margin - boxH;
        if (boxX < 4) boxX = 4;
        if (boxY < 4) boxY = 4;

        Color bg = isDark
                ? Color.color(0.10, 0.10, 0.12, 0.92)
                : Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.94);
        Color border = isDark
                ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.10)
                : Color.color(0, 0, 0, 0.10);
        Color fg = isDark
                ? Color.color(0.96, 0.96, 0.98, 1)
                : Color.color(0.11, 0.11, 0.12, 1);

        gc.setFill(bg);
        gc.fillRoundRect(boxX, boxY, boxW, boxH, 8, 8);
        gc.setStroke(border);
        gc.setLineWidth(1);
        gc.strokeRoundRect(boxX + 0.5, boxY + 0.5, boxW - 1, boxH - 1, 8, 8);

        gc.setFill(fg);
        gc.setTextAlign(TextAlignment.LEFT);
        gc.fillText(label, boxX + padX, boxY + padY + 10);
    }

    private double textWidth(GraphicsContext gc, String s) {
        return gc.getFont().getSize() * s.length() * 0.55;
    }

    private void drawBand(GraphicsContext gc, MusicBand band, RenderState state, boolean isDark) {
        double offsetX = panelWidth / 2.0;
        double offsetY = panelHeight / 2.0;
        double effectiveScale = scale * zoom;
        double screenX = state.x * effectiveScale + offsetX + panX;
        double screenY = -state.y * effectiveScale + offsetY + panY;
        double r = scaledRadius(band) * zoom;

        if (screenX + r < -200 || screenX - r > panelWidth + 200) return;
        if (screenY + r < -200 || screenY - r > panelHeight + 200) return;

        boolean isHovered = hoveredBandId == band.getId();
        boolean isSelected = selectedBandId == band.getId();
        String color = colorManager.getColorForUser(band.getOwnerLogin());

        gc.save();
        gc.setGlobalAlpha(state.opacity);
        gc.translate(screenX, screenY);
        gc.scale(state.scale, state.scale);

        Color base = Color.web(color);

        // Soft outer glow ring
        double pulse = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(pulsePhase);
        double glowR = r + 6 + pulse * 4;
        gc.setFill(base.deriveColor(0, 1, 1, 0.18));
        gc.fillOval(-glowR, -glowR, glowR * 2, glowR * 2);

        // Colored drop shadow
        javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow ds = new javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow();
        ds.setColor(base.deriveColor(0, 1, 0.55, isHovered || isSelected ? 0.65 : 0.40));
        ds.setRadius(isHovered || isSelected ? 26 : 14);
        ds.setOffsetY(isHovered || isSelected ? 6 : 3);
        gc.setEffect(ds);

        // Glossy radial gradient
        double gradR = isHovered || isSelected ? r * 0.4 : r * 0.3;
        javafx.scene.paint.RadialGradient gradient = new javafx.scene.paint.RadialGradient(
                0, 0, -r * 0.35, -r * 0.35, r, false, javafx.scene.paint.CycleMethod.NO_CYCLE,
                new javafx.scene.paint.Stop(0, base.deriveColor(0, 1.1, 1.25, 1)),
                new javafx.scene.paint.Stop(gradR / r, base),
                new javafx.scene.paint.Stop(1, base.deriveColor(0, 0.85, 0.62, 1))
        );
        gc.setFill(gradient);
        gc.fillOval(-r, -r, r * 2, r * 2);

        if (isSelected) {
            gc.setEffect(null);
            gc.setStroke(base.brighter());
            gc.setLineWidth(3);
            double hr = r + 6;
            gc.strokeOval(-hr, -hr, hr * 2, hr * 2);
            gc.setLineWidth(1.5);
            double hr2 = r + 12;
            gc.setStroke(base.deriveColor(0, 1, 1, 0.5));
            gc.strokeOval(-hr2, -hr2, hr2 * 2, hr2 * 2);
        }

        // Rim
        gc.setEffect(null);
        gc.setStroke(isDark ? Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.85) : Color.color(1, 1, 1, 0.95));
        gc.setLineWidth(isSelected ? 2 : (isHovered ? 2 : 1.2));
        gc.strokeOval(-r, -r, r * 2, r * 2);

        // Label
        if (r > 20 || isHovered) {
            javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow labelDs = new javafx.scene.effect.DropShadow();
            labelDs.setColor(Color.color(0, 0, 0, 0.55));
            labelDs.setRadius(3);
            labelDs.setOffsetY(1);
            gc.setEffect(labelDs);
            gc.setFill(Color.WHITE);
            gc.setFont(Font.font("Inter", FontWeight.SEMI_BOLD, 11));
            gc.setTextAlign(TextAlignment.CENTER);
            String name = band.getName() == null ? "?" : band.getName();
            String shortName = name.length() > 10 ? name.substring(0, 8) + "â€¦" : name;
            gc.fillText(shortName, 0, 4);
            gc.setEffect(null);
        }

        String owner = band.getOwnerLogin();
        String currentUser = appState.getCurrentUser();
        if (owner != null && owner.equals(currentUser)) {
            double dotR = 5;
            double dotX = r * 0.65;
            double dotY = -r * 0.85;
            gc.setFill(Color.WHITE);
            gc.fillOval(dotX - dotR, dotY - dotR, dotR * 2, dotR * 2);
            gc.setFill(Color.web("#007AFF"));
            double inner = 2.5;
            gc.fillOval(dotX - inner, dotY - inner, inner * 2, inner * 2);
        }

        gc.restore();
    }

    private void showDetailsPopup(long bandId) {
        MusicBand band = findBandById(bandId);
        if (band == null) return;

        detailsContent.getChildren().clear();
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        String color = colorManager.getColorForUser(band.getOwnerLogin());

        addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_ID), String.valueOf(band.getId()), true);
        addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_NAME), band.getName(), false);
        addDetailRowWithColor(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_OWNER),
                band.getOwnerLogin() == null ? "â€”" : band.getOwnerLogin(), color, false);
        addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_GENRE),
                band.getGenre() == null ? "â€”" : band.getGenre().name(), false);
        addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_PARTICIPANTS),
                UiText.integerFormat(locale).format(band.getNumberOfParticipants()), false);
        addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COORDINATES),
                UiText.decimalFormat(locale, 1, 1).format(band.getCoordinates().getX()) + ", "
                        + UiText.decimalFormat(locale, 1, 1).format(band.getCoordinates().getY()),
                true);
        if (band.getStudio() != null) {
            addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_STUDIO), band.getStudio().getName(), false);
        }
        if (band.getSinglesCount() != null) {
            addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_SINGLES),
                    UiText.integerFormat(locale).format(band.getSinglesCount()), false);
        }
        if (band.getEstablishmentDate() != null) {
            addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_DATE),
                    UiText.dateFormat(locale).format(
                            java.util.Date.from(band.getEstablishmentDate()
                                    .atStartOfDay(java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()).toInstant())),
                    false);
        }
        if (band.getCreationDate() != null) {
            addDetailRow(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FIELD_CREATION),
                    UiText.dateTimeFormat(locale).format(band.getCreationDate()), false);
        }

        boolean isOwner = band.getOwnerLogin() != null
                && band.getOwnerLogin().equals(appState.getCurrentUser());
        editButton.setVisible(isOwner);
        editButton.setManaged(isOwner);
        deleteButton.setVisible(isOwner);
        deleteButton.setManaged(isOwner);
        if (isOwner) {
            applyLocale(locale);
            Region spacer = new Region();
            HBox.setHgrow(spacer, Priority.ALWAYS);
            HBox actions = new HBox(8, spacer, editButton, deleteButton);
            actions.getStyleClass().add("details-actions");
            actions.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_RIGHT);
            detailsContent.getChildren().add(actions);
        }

        detailsPopup.setVisible(true);
        detailsPopup.setManaged(true);
    }

    private void hideDetailsPopup() {
        detailsPopup.setVisible(false);
        detailsPopup.setManaged(false);
    }

    private void addDetailRow(String label, String value, boolean monospace) {
        HBox row = new HBox(8);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.getStyleClass().add("details-row");
        Label lblLabel = new Label(label);
        lblLabel.getStyleClass().add("detail-label");
        lblLabel.setPrefWidth(110);
        Label lblValue = new Label(value);
        if (monospace) {
            lblValue.getStyleClass().addAll("detail-value", "value-mono");
        } else {
            lblValue.getStyleClass().add("detail-value");
        }
        row.getChildren().addAll(lblLabel, lblValue);
        detailsContent.getChildren().add(row);
    }

    private void addDetailRowWithColor(String label, String value, String color, boolean monospace) {
        HBox row = new HBox(8);
        row.setAlignment(Pos.CENTER_LEFT);
        row.getStyleClass().add("details-row");
        Label lblLabel = new Label(label);
        lblLabel.getStyleClass().add("detail-label");
        lblLabel.setPrefWidth(110);
        javafx.scene.shape.Circle dot = new javafx.scene.shape.Circle(5);
        dot.setFill(Color.web(color));
        dot.getStyleClass().add("owner-color-dot");
        Label lblValue = new Label(value);
        lblValue.getStyleClass().add("detail-value");
        row.getChildren().addAll(lblLabel, dot, lblValue);
        detailsContent.getChildren().add(row);
    }

    private MusicBand findBandById(long id) {
        for (MusicBand b : appState.getItems()) {
            if (b != null && b.getId() == id) return b;
        }
        return null;
    }

    public void setSelectedBand(MusicBand band) {
        if (band == null) {
            selectedBandId = -1L;
            selectedBand = null;
            hideDetailsPopup();
        } else {
            selectedBandId = band.getId();
            selectedBand = band;
            showDetailsPopup(band.getId());
        }
    }

    public MusicBand getSelectedBand() {
        return selectedBand;
    }

    public Button getEditButton() {
        return editButton;
    }

    public Button getDeleteButton() {
        return deleteButton;
    }

    private static class RenderState {
        double x;
        double y;
        double scale = 0;
        double opacity = 0;

        RenderState(double x, double y) {
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
        }
    }
}

package client.javafx;

import javafx.scene.Node;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Dialog;
import javafx.scene.control.DialogPane;

/**
 * Theme propagation for {@link Dialog}s.  JavaFX renders each dialog in
 * its own stage and therefore loses the {@code theme-*} CSS class that
 * lives on the main scene's root.  Without it, the default modena look
 * bleeds through inside the dialog.  This helper copies the class over
 * to the dialog's root node and ensures the main stylesheet is loaded.
 */
public final class DialogThemer {

    private DialogThemer() {}

    public static void applyTheme(Dialog<?> dialog, String theme, String stylesheet) {
        if (dialog == null) return;
        DialogPane pane = dialog.getDialogPane();
        if (pane == null) return;
        if (stylesheet != null && !pane.getStylesheets().contains(stylesheet)) {
            pane.getStylesheets().add(stylesheet);
        }
        pane.getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        pane.getStyleClass().add("theme-" + (theme == null ? "light" : theme));
        pane.sceneProperty().addListener((obs, oldScene, newScene) -> {
            if (newScene != null) {
                copyThemeToScene(newScene, theme);
                if (stylesheet != null && !newScene.getStylesheets().contains(stylesheet)) {
                    newScene.getStylesheets().add(stylesheet);
                }
            }
        });
        Scene current = pane.getScene();
        if (current != null) copyThemeToScene(current, theme);
    }

    private static void copyThemeToScene(Scene scene, String theme) {
        if (scene == null || scene.getRoot() == null) return;
        Node root = scene.getRoot();
        root.getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        root.getStyleClass().add("theme-" + (theme == null ? "light" : theme));
    }
}

package client.javafx;

import client.ClientCommandRegistry;
import client.javafx.components.BandEditDialog;
import client.javafx.components.BandTableView;
import client.javafx.components.CommandPalette;
import client.javafx.components.HelpDialog;
import client.javafx.components.SidebarPane;

import client.javafx.components.TopBarPane;
import client.javafx.components.VisualizationCanvas;
import client.javafx.network.BandService;
import client.javafx.sync.SyncService;
import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.i18n.UiText;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import common.network.CommandMeta;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.fxml.FXML;
import javafx.scene.Scene;
import javafx.scene.control.Alert;
import javafx.scene.control.Button;
import javafx.scene.control.ButtonType;

public class MainController {

    @FXML private BorderPane root;

    @FXML private Label vizTitle;
    @FXML private VBox detailsPopup;

    @FXML private VBox detailsContent;

    @FXML private VBox toastPane;

    private AppState appState;
    private SidebarPane sidebar;
    private TopBarPane topBar;
    private BandTableView bandTableView;
    private VisualizationCanvas visualizationCanvas;
    private ToastPane toasts;

    private SyncService syncService;

    private AppState.SyncDiff lastReportedDiff = new AppState.SyncDiff(0, 0, 0);

    public void init(AppState appState) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.bandService = new BandService(appState);

        buildSidebar();
        buildTopBar();
        buildTable();
        buildVisualization();
        wireSplitPane();
        buildToasts();
        wireActions();
        installKeyboardShortcuts();
        applyLocale(appState.getLocale());
        applyTheme(appState.getTheme());
        bindStatusBar();
        startSync();
        updateTableTitle();

        root.setOpacity(0);
        root.setTranslateY(8);
        javafx.animation.FadeTransition fade = new javafx.animation.FadeTransition(
                javafx.util.Duration.millis(420), root);
        fade.setFromValue(0);
        fade.setToValue(1);
        javafx.animation.TranslateTransition slide = new javafx.animation.TranslateTransition(
                javafx.util.Duration.millis(420), root);
        slide.setFromY(8);
        slide.setToY(0);
        javafx.animation.Interpolator ease = javafx.animation.Interpolator.EASE_OUT;
        fade.setInterpolator(ease);
        slide.setInterpolator(ease);
        fade.play();
        slide.play();

        appState.getItems().addListener((javafx.collections.ListChangeListener<common.models.MusicBand>) c ->
                Platform.runLater(this::updateTableTitle));
        appState.localeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                Platform.runLater(() -> applyLocale(newVal)));
        appState.themeProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                Platform.runLater(() -> applyTheme(newVal)));
        appState.lastSyncDiffProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) ->
                Platform.runLater(() -> onSyncDiff(newVal)));
    }

    @FXML

    }

    private void buildSidebar() {
        sidebar = new SidebarPane(appState);
        sidebarPane.getChildren().setAll(sidebar);
    }

    private void buildTopBar() {

        topBarPane.getChildren().setAll(topBar);

    private void buildTable() {

        VBox.setVgrow(bandTableView, javafx.scene.layout.Priority.ALWAYS);
        tableContainer.getChildren().add(bandTableView);
    }

    private void buildVisualization() {
        visualizationCanvas = new VisualizationCanvas(appState, detailsPopup, detailsContent);
        vizStackPane.setPrefSize(Double.MAX_VALUE, Double.MAX_VALUE);
        vizStackPane.setMinSize(0, 0);
        vizStackPane.getChildren().add(0, visualizationCanvas);
        visualizationCanvas.bindToParentSize(vizStackPane);
    }

    private void wireSplitPane() {
        if (centerSplitPane == null) return;
        for (javafx.scene.Node pane : centerSplitPane.getItems()) {
            SplitPane.setResizableWithParent(pane, true);
        }
        Runnable fixDividers = () -> {
            if (centerSplitPane.getScene() == null) return;
            for (javafx.scene.Node n : centerSplitPane.getScene().getRoot().lookupAll(".split-pane-divider")) {
                n.setPickOnBounds(true);
            }
        };
        centerSplitPane.sceneProperty().addListener((obs, o, n) -> {
            if (n != null) {
                Platform.runLater(fixDividers);
            }
        });
        Platform.runLater(fixDividers);
    }

    private void buildToasts() {
        toasts = new ToastPane();
        if (toastPane != null) {
            toastPane.getChildren().add(toasts);
        }
    }

    private void wireActions() {
        if (topBar != null) {
            topBar.getAddButton().setOnAction(e -> openCreateDialog());
            topBar.getCommandsButton().setOnAction(e -> openCommandPalette());
        }
        if (sidebar != null) {
            sidebar.getLogoutButton().setOnAction(e -> handleLogout());
        }
        if (bandTableView != null) {
            bandTableView.addEventHandler(BandTableView.BandEditEvent.EDIT, this::onBandEditEvent);
            bandTableView.addEventHandler(BandTableView.BandDeleteEvent.DELETE, this::onBandDeleteEvent);
            bandTableView.addEventHandler(BandTableView.BandRemoveGreaterEvent.REMOVE_GREATER, this::onBandRemoveGreater);
            bandTableView.addEventHandler(BandTableView.BandRemoveLowerEvent.REMOVE_LOWER, this::onBandRemoveLower);
        }
        if (visualizationCanvas != null) {
            visualizationCanvas.getEditButton().setOnAction(e -> onCanvasEdit());
            visualizationCanvas.getDeleteButton().setOnAction(e -> onCanvasDelete());
        }
    }

    private void installKeyboardShortcuts() {
        Scene scene = root.getScene();
        if (scene == null) {
            root.sceneProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> {
                if (newVal != null) attachSceneHandlers(newVal);
            });
        } else {
            attachSceneHandlers(scene);
        }
    }

    private void attachSceneHandlers(Scene scene) {
        scene.addEventFilter(KeyEvent.KEY_PRESSED, this::onKeyPressed);
    }

    private void onKeyPressed(KeyEvent event) {
        if (event.isConsumed()) return;
        boolean ctrl = event.isControlDown() || event.isMetaDown();

        if (ctrl && event.getCode() == KeyCode.N) {
            event.consume();
            openCreateDialog();
            return;
        }
        if (ctrl && event.getCode() == KeyCode.F) {
            event.consume();
            if (filterField != null) filterField.requestFocus();
            return;
        }
        if (ctrl && event.getCode() == KeyCode.K) {
            event.consume();
            openCommandPalette();
            return;
        }
        if (ctrl && event.getCode() == KeyCode.L) {
            event.consume();
            handleLogout();
            return;
        }
        if (event.getCode() == KeyCode.F1) {
            event.consume();
            openHelpDialog();
            return;
        }
        if (event.getCode() == KeyCode.DELETE && isTableFocused()) {
            MusicBand selected = bandTableView == null ? null : bandTableView.getSelectedBand();
            if (selected != null) {
                event.consume();
                confirmAndDelete(selected);
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean isTableFocused() {
        javafx.scene.Node focus = root.getScene() == null ? null : root.getScene().getFocusOwner();
        if (focus == null || bandTableView == null) return false;
        if (focus == bandTableView) return true;
        javafx.scene.Node n = focus.getParent();
        while (n != null) {
            if (n == bandTableView) return true;
            n = n.getParent();
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void bindStatusBar() {
        if (statusLabel == null) return;
        statusLabel.textProperty().bind(appState.statusMessageProperty());
        appState.statusKindProperty().addListener((obs, oldVal, newVal) -> updateStatusKindClass());
        updateStatusKindClass();
    }

    private void updateStatusKindClass() {
        if (statusLabel == null) return;
        statusLabel.getStyleClass().removeAll("status-info", "status-success", "status-error");
        String kind = appState.getStatusKind();
        if (kind != null && !kind.isBlank()) {
            statusLabel.getStyleClass().add("status-" + kind);
        }
    }

    private void startSync() {
        syncService = new SyncService(appState, new ClientCommandRegistry(), root);
        syncService.start();
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        if (syncService != null) syncService.stop();
    }

    public BandTableView getBandTableView() {
        return bandTableView;
    }

    public VisualizationCanvas getVisualizationCanvas() {
        return visualizationCanvas;
    }

    private void applyLocale(Locale locale) {
        updateTableTitle();
        if (vizTitle != null) vizTitle.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.VISUALIZATION));
        if (filterField != null) filterField.setPromptText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.FILTER));
        if (detailsTitle != null) detailsTitle.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.DETAILS));
        if (sidebar != null) sidebar.applyLocale(locale);
        if (topBar != null) topBar.applyLocale(locale);
        if (visualizationCanvas != null) visualizationCanvas.applyLocale(locale);
        if (bandTableView != null) bandTableView.applyLocale();
    }

    private void updateTableTitle() {
        if (tableTitle == null) return;
        int count = appState.getItems().size();
        String base = UiText.text(appState.getLocale(), UiText.Key.ITEMS);
        tableTitle.setText(base + "  Â·  " + count);
    }

    private void applyTheme(String theme) {
        root.getStyleClass().removeAll("theme-light", "theme-dark");
        root.getStyleClass().add("theme-" + theme);
        if (bandTableView != null) bandTableView.applyTheme(theme);
        if (visualizationCanvas != null) visualizationCanvas.applyTheme(theme);
        if (sidebar != null) sidebar.applyTheme(theme);
        if (topBar != null) topBar.applyTheme(theme);
    }

    public void refreshData() {
        if (bandTableView != null) bandTableView.refresh();
        if (visualizationCanvas != null) visualizationCanvas.refresh();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Action handlers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private void onSyncDiff(AppState.SyncDiff diff) {
        if (diff == null || toasts == null) return;
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        if (diff.added > 0) {
            String msg = String.format(
                    locale, UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.TOAST_BANDS_ADDED), diff.added);
            toasts.show(msg, ToastPane.Kind.INFO);
        }
        if (diff.removed > 0) {
            String msg = String.format(
                    locale, UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.TOAST_BANDS_REMOVED), diff.removed);
            toasts.show(msg, ToastPane.Kind.WARNING);
        }
        lastReportedDiff = diff;
    }

    private void openCreateDialog() {
        BandEditDialog dialog = new BandEditDialog(appState, null);
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(dialog, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        MusicBand result = dialog.showAndWait().orElse(null);
        if (result == null) return;
        String key = generateKey();
        bandService.insert(key, result)
                .whenComplete((res, ex) -> Platform.runLater(() -> reportResult(res, ex)));
    }

    private void onBandEditEvent(BandTableView.BandEditEvent event) {
        openEditDialog(event.getBand());
    }

    private void onCanvasEdit() {
        MusicBand band = visualizationCanvas == null ? null : visualizationCanvas.getSelectedBand();
        if (band != null) openEditDialog(band);
    }

    private void openEditDialog(MusicBand existing) {
        BandEditDialog dialog = new BandEditDialog(appState, existing);
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(dialog, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        MusicBand updated = dialog.showAndWait().orElse(null);
        if (updated == null) return;
        bandService.update(existing.getId(), updated)
                .whenComplete((res, ex) -> Platform.runLater(() -> reportResult(res, ex)));
    }

    private void onBandDeleteEvent(BandTableView.BandDeleteEvent event) {
        confirmAndDelete(event.getBand());
    }

    private void onCanvasDelete() {
        MusicBand band = visualizationCanvas == null ? null : visualizationCanvas.getSelectedBand();
        if (band != null) confirmAndDelete(band);
    }

    private void confirmAndDelete(MusicBand band) {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
                UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONFIRM_DELETE_MESSAGE) + "\n\n" + band.getName(),
                ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        alert.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.CONFIRM_DELETE_TITLE));
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(alert, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        alert.showAndWait().ifPresent(bt -> {
            if (bt == ButtonType.YES) {
                String key = appState.getKeyForId(band.getId());
                if (key == null) {
                    appState.setStatus(
                            UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + "key not found",
                            "error");
                    return;
                }
                bandService.removeKey(key)
                        .whenComplete((res, ex) -> Platform.runLater(() -> reportResult(res, ex)));
            }
        });
    }

    private void onBandRemoveGreater(BandTableView.BandRemoveGreaterEvent event) {
        runReferenceCommand(event.getBand(), true);
    }

    private void onBandRemoveLower(BandTableView.BandRemoveLowerEvent event) {
        runReferenceCommand(event.getBand(), false);
    }

    private void runReferenceCommand(MusicBand reference, boolean greater) {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
                (greater
                        ? UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_GREATER)
                        : UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMAND_REMOVE_LOWER))
                        + " â€” " + reference.getName(),
                ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        alert.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(alert, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        alert.showAndWait().ifPresent(bt -> {
            if (bt != ButtonType.YES) return;
            var future = greater
                    ? bandService.removeGreater(reference)
                    : bandService.removeLower(reference);
            future.whenComplete((res, ex) -> Platform.runLater(() -> reportResult(res, ex)));
        });
    }

    private void openCommandPalette() {
        CommandPalette palette = new CommandPalette(appState, bandService, this::onPaletteResult);
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(palette, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        Scene scene = root.getScene();
        if (scene != null && scene.getWindow() instanceof Stage stage) {
            palette.initOwner(stage);
        }
        palette.show();
    }

    private void openHelpDialog() {
        List<CommandMeta> commands = new ClientCommandRegistry().exportPublicCommandMeta();
        HelpDialog dialog = new HelpDialog(appState, commands);
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(dialog, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        Scene scene = root.getScene();
        if (scene != null && scene.getWindow() instanceof Stage stage) {
            dialog.initOwner(stage);
        }
        dialog.showAndWait();
    }

    private void onPaletteResult(CommandResult result) {
        reportResult(result, null);
    }

    private void reportResult(CommandResult res, Throwable ex) {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        if (ex != null) {
            String msg = UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + ex.getMessage();
            showResult(locale, msg, "error");
            return;
        }
        if (res == null) {
            String msg = UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + "no result";
            showResult(locale, msg, "error");
            return;
        }
        if (!res.isSuccess()) {
            String msg = UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED) + res.getMessage();
            showResult(locale, msg, "error");
        } else {
            String msg = UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_SUCCESS) + " â€” " + res.getMessage();
            showResult(locale, msg, "success");
        }
    }

    /**
     * Show a command result.  Short messages go in the status bar + toast;
     * long messages (help text, info dump, group-by-name map, etc.) are
     * routed to a scrollable Alert dialog so the main view doesn't grow.
     */
    private void showResult(Locale locale, String message, String kind) {
        if (message == null) message = "";
        String shortMsg = message;
        final int SOFT_LIMIT = 180;
        if (shortMsg.length() > SOFT_LIMIT) {
            shortMsg = shortMsg.substring(0, SOFT_LIMIT - 1) + "â€¦";
        }
        appState.setStatus(shortMsg, kind);
        if (toasts != null) toasts.show(shortMsg, ToastPane.Kind.valueOf(kind.toUpperCase()));

        if (message.length() > SOFT_LIMIT) {
            showLongResultDialog(locale, message, kind);
        }
    }

    private void showLongResultDialog(Locale locale, String message, String kind) {
        javafx.scene.control.Alert alert = new javafx.scene.control.Alert(
                javafx.scene.control.Alert.AlertType.INFORMATION);
        alert.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.COMMANDS));
        alert.setHeaderText(kind.equals("error")
                ? UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_FAILED)
                : UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.OPERATION_SUCCESS));
        javafx.scene.control.TextArea area = new javafx.scene.control.TextArea(message);
        area.setEditable(false);
        area.setWrapText(true);
        area.setPrefSize(640, 360);
        area.getStyleClass().add("result-text-area");
        alert.getDialogPane().setContent(area);
        alert.getDialogPane().setPrefSize(720, 460);
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(alert, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        Scene scene = root.getScene();
        if (scene != null && scene.getWindow() instanceof Stage stage) {
            alert.initOwner(stage);
        }
        alert.showAndWait();
    }

    private void handleLogout() {
        Locale locale = appState.getLocale();
        Alert alert = new Alert(Alert.AlertType.CONFIRMATION,
                UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.LOGOUT) + "?",
                ButtonType.YES, ButtonType.NO);
        alert.setTitle(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.LOGOUT));
        Button yes = (Button) alert.getDialogPane().lookupButton(ButtonType.YES);
        yes.setText(UiText.text(locale, UiText.Key.LOGOUT));
        DialogThemer.applyTheme(alert, appState.getTheme(), "/css/main.css");
        alert.showAndWait().ifPresent(bt -> {
            if (bt == ButtonType.YES) {
                shutdown();
                appState.clearData();
                appState.setCredentials(null);
                returnToLogin();
            }
        });
    }

    private void returnToLogin() {
        try {
            javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader loader = new javafx.fxml.FXMLLoader(
                    getClass().getResource("/fxml/LoginView.fxml"));
            javafx.scene.Parent loginRoot = loader.load();
            client.ui.LoginController controller = loader.getController();
            controller.init(appState);
            Stage stage = (Stage) root.getScene().getWindow();
            Scene scene = new Scene(loginRoot, stage.getWidth(), stage.getHeight());
            stage.setScene(scene);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    private static String generateKey() {
        return "k-" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().substring(0, 8);
    }
}

package client.javafx.model;

import client.ui.AppState;
import javafx.collections.ObservableMap;

public class BandColorManager {

    private static final String[] PREDEFINED_COLORS = {
            "#0052CC",
            "#00875A",
            "#DE350B",
            "#FF991F",
            "#6554C0",
            "#00B8D9",
            "#36B37E"
    };

    private final AppState appState;

    public BandColorManager(AppState appState) {
        this.appState = appState;
    }

    public String getColorForUser(String login) {
        if (login == null || login.isBlank()) {
            return "#94A3B8";
        }
        ObservableMap<String, String> map = appState.getUserColorMap();
        if (map.containsKey(login)) {
            return map.get(login);
        }
        int colorIndex = map.size() % PREDEFINED_COLORS.length;
        String color = PREDEFINED_COLORS[colorIndex];
        map.put(login, color);
        return color;
    }

    public static String[] getAllColors() {
        return PREDEFINED_COLORS.clone();
    }
}

package client.javafx.network;

import client.ui.AppState;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandRequest;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;

import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;

/**
 * High-level wrapper around the 13 server commands. Each method submits a
 * command on {@link AppState#getExecutor()} and returns a
 * {@link CompletableFuture} that completes with the server's
 * {@link CommandResult}. UI code can chain a success/failure handler without
 * having to deal with the raw TCP layer.
 */
public class BandService {

    private final AppState appState;
    private final CommandClient client;
    private final Executor executor;

    public BandService(AppState appState) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.client = new CommandClient(appState);
        this.executor = appState.getExecutor();
    }

    private CompletableFuture<CommandResult> run(String commandName, CommandRequest request) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            try {
                return client.send(commandName, request);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return new CommandResult("Network error: " + e.getMessage(), false);
            }
        }, executor);
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> show() {
        return run(CommandNames.SHOW, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> info() {
        return run(CommandNames.INFO, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> help() {
        return run(CommandNames.HELP, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> insert(String key, MusicBand band) {
        return run(CommandNames.INSERT, new CommandRequest(key, band, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> update(long id, MusicBand band) {
        return run(CommandNames.UPDATE, new CommandRequest(String.valueOf(id), band, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> removeKey(String key) {
        return run(CommandNames.REMOVE_KEY, new CommandRequest(key, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> removeGreater(MusicBand reference) {
        return run(CommandNames.REMOVE_GREATER, new CommandRequest("", reference, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> removeGreaterKey(String key) {
        return run(CommandNames.REMOVE_GREATER_KEY, new CommandRequest(key, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> removeLower(MusicBand reference) {
        return run(CommandNames.REMOVE_LOWER, new CommandRequest("", reference, credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> clear() {
        return run(CommandNames.CLEAR, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> countByNumberOfParticipants(long n) {
        return run(CommandNames.COUNT_BY_PARTICIPANTS, new CommandRequest(String.valueOf(n), credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> groupCountingByName() {
        return run(CommandNames.GROUP_COUNTING_BY_NAME, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    public CompletableFuture<CommandResult> printFieldDescendingSinglesCount() {
        return run(CommandNames.PRINT_FIELD_DESCENDING_SINGLES_COUNT, new CommandRequest("", credentials()));
    }

    private common.network.AuthCredentials credentials() {
        return appState.getCredentials();
    }
}

package client.javafx.network;

import client.NonBlockingTcpClient;
import client.ui.AppState;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandRequest;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;

/**
 * Sends a single command packet to the server and returns the result.
 * Should be invoked on a background thread (e.g. {@code AppState.getExecutor()}).
 */
public final class CommandClient {

    private final AppState appState;
    private final int timeoutMillis;

    public CommandClient(AppState appState) {
        this(appState, 5000);
    }

    public CommandClient(AppState appState, int timeoutMillis) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.timeoutMillis = timeoutMillis;
    }

    public CommandResult send(String commandName, CommandRequest request) throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
        if (request.getCredentials() == null && appState.getCredentials() != null) {
            request = new CommandRequest(
                    request.getArgument(),
                    request.getBand(),
                    appState.getCredentials()
            );
        }
        CommandPacket packet = new CommandPacket(commandName, request);
        NonBlockingTcpClient client = new NonBlockingTcpClient(appState.getHost(), appState.getPort());
        ResponsePacket response = client.send(packet, timeoutMillis);
        return response.getResult();
    }

    public Callable<CommandResult> asCallable(String commandName, CommandRequest request) {
        return () -> send(commandName, request);
    }
}

    public static final String REMOVE_KEY = "remove_key";
    public static final String REMOVE_GREATER = "remove_greater";
    public static final String REMOVE_GREATER_KEY = "remove_greater_key";
    public static final String REMOVE_LOWER = "remove_lower";
    public static final String CLEAR = "clear";
    public static final String GROUP_COUNTING_BY_NAME = "group_counting_by_name";
    public static final String COUNT_BY_PARTICIPANTS = "count_by_number_of_participants";
    public static final String PRINT_FIELD_DESCENDING_SINGLES_COUNT = "print_field_descending_singles_count";

    private CommandNames() {}
}

package client.javafx.sync;

import client.ClientCommandRegistry;
import client.NonBlockingTcpClient;
import client.javafx.network.CommandNames;
import client.ui.AppState;
import client.ui.AppState.ConnectionState;
import client.ui.AppState.SyncDiff;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandRequest;
import common.commandsabstraction.CommandResult;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.network.CommandMeta;
import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.ProtocolConstants;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;
import javafx.application.Platform;
import javafx.collections.ObservableList;
import javafx.scene.layout.BorderPane;

import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/**
 * Background service that periodically polls the server:
 *   1. {@code __get_commands__}  - refreshes the command metadata in ClientCommandRegistry
 *   2. {@code show}              - refreshes the actual band collection into AppState.items
 *
 * Also tracks connection state and the diff between successive polls so the UI can show
 * real-time "X added, Y removed" toasts and a connection status indicator.
 */
public class SyncService {
    private static final Logger LOGGER = Logger.getLogger(SyncService.class.getName());
    private static final int POLL_INTERVAL_MILLIS = 3000;
    private static final int METADATA_TIMEOUT_MILLIS = 5000;
    private static final int DATA_TIMEOUT_MILLIS = 5000;
    private static final int RECONNECT_FAIL_THRESHOLD = 2;

    private final AppState appState;
    private final ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry;
    private final BorderPane root;
    private final Runnable onConnected;
    private final AtomicBoolean lastDataPollSucceeded = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final AtomicBoolean lastMetadataPollSucceeded = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final Set<Long> lastSeenIds = new HashSet<>();
    private int consecutiveFailures = 0;
    private volatile boolean running = true;
    private Thread worker;

    public SyncService(AppState appState, ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry, BorderPane root) {
        this(appState, commandRegistry, root, null);
    }

    public SyncService(AppState appState, ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry,
                       BorderPane root, Runnable onConnected) {
        this.appState = appState;
        this.commandRegistry = commandRegistry;
        this.root = root;
        this.onConnected = onConnected;
    }

    public void start() {
        if (worker != null) return;
        consecutiveFailures = 0;
        lastSeenIds.clear();
        lastDataPollSucceeded.set(false);
        lastMetadataPollSucceeded.set(false);
        Platform.runLater(() -> appState.setConnectionState(ConnectionState.CONNECTING));
        worker = new Thread(this::runLoop, "client-sync-worker");
        worker.setDaemon(true);
        worker.start();
    }

    public void stop() {
        running = false;
        if (worker != null) worker.interrupt();
    }

    private void runLoop() {
        boolean firstPoll = true;
        while (running) {
            try {
                if (appState.getCredentials() != null) {
                    pollMetadata();
                    pollData();
                    boolean ok = lastDataPollSucceeded.get() || lastMetadataPollSucceeded.get();
                    if (ok) {
                        if (consecutiveFailures >= RECONNECT_FAIL_THRESHOLD) {
                            Platform.runLater(() -> appState.setConnectionState(ConnectionState.CONNECTED));
                        }
                        consecutiveFailures = 0;
                        if (firstPoll) {
                            firstPoll = false;
                            Platform.runLater(() -> appState.setConnectionState(ConnectionState.CONNECTED));
                            if (onConnected != null) {
                                Platform.runLater(onConnected);
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        consecutiveFailures++;
                        if (consecutiveFailures == RECONNECT_FAIL_THRESHOLD) {
                            Platform.runLater(() -> appState.setConnectionState(ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED));
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception e) {
                if (running) {
                    LOGGER.log(Level.WARNING, "Sync error", e);
                }
            }
            try {
                Thread.sleep(POLL_INTERVAL_MILLIS);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private void pollMetadata() {
        try {
            NonBlockingTcpClient client = new NonBlockingTcpClient(appState.getHost(), appState.getPort());
            CommandPacket packet = new CommandPacket(
                    ProtocolConstants.GET_COMMANDS,
                    new CommandRequest("", appState.getCredentials()));
            ResponsePacket response = client.send(packet, METADATA_TIMEOUT_MILLIS);
            CommandResult result = response.getResult();
            lastMetadataPollSucceeded.set(result.isSuccess());
            if (result.isSuccess() && result.getData() instanceof Map<?, ?> raw) {
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                Map<String, CommandMeta> meta = (Map<String, CommandMeta>) raw;
                Platform.runLater(() -> commandRegistry.applyServerCommands(meta));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastMetadataPollSucceeded.set(false);
            LOGGER.log(Level.FINE, "Metadata poll failed", e);
        }
    }

    private void pollData() {
        try {
            NonBlockingTcpClient client = new NonBlockingTcpClient(appState.getHost(), appState.getPort());
            CommandPacket packet = new CommandPacket(
                    CommandNames.SHOW,
                    new CommandRequest("", appState.getCredentials()));
            ResponsePacket response = client.send(packet, DATA_TIMEOUT_MILLIS);
            CommandResult result = response.getResult();
            lastDataPollSucceeded.set(result.isSuccess());
            if (result.isSuccess() && result.getData() instanceof Map<?, ?> raw) {
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                Map<String, MusicBand> entries = (Map<String, MusicBand>) raw;
                Platform.runLater(() -> updateItems(entries));
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            lastDataPollSucceeded.set(false);
            LOGGER.log(Level.FINE, "Data poll failed", e);
        }
    }

    private void updateItems(Map<String, MusicBand> entries) {
        appState.setIdToKey(entries);

        Set<Long> newIds = new HashSet<>();
        int added = 0;
        for (MusicBand b : entries.values()) {
            if (b != null) {
                long id = b.getId();
                newIds.add(id);
                if (!lastSeenIds.contains(id)) {
                    added++;
                }
            }
        }
        int removed = 0;
        for (Long oldId : lastSeenIds) {
            if (!newIds.contains(oldId)) removed++;
        }
        lastSeenIds.clear();
        lastSeenIds.addAll(newIds);

        ObservableList<MusicBand> target = appState.getItems();
        target.setAll(entries.values());

        SyncDiff diff = new SyncDiff(added, removed, 0);
        appState.setLastSyncDiff(diff);
    }
}

package client;

import common.network.CommandPacket;
import common.network.PacketCodec;
import common.network.ProtocolConstants;
import common.network.ResponsePacket;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.SocketTimeoutException;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.channels.SelectionKey;
import java.nio.channels.Selector;
import java.nio.channels.SocketChannel;
import java.util.Iterator;

/**
 * Non-blocking TCP client for sending commands to server.
 */
public class NonBlockingTcpClient {
    private static final int MAX_PACKET_BYTES = ProtocolConstants.MAX_PACKET_BYTES;

    private final String host;
    private final int port;

    public NonBlockingTcpClient(String host, int port) {
        this.host = host;
        this.port = port;
    }

    /**
     * Send a command packet and wait for response.
     */
    public ResponsePacket send(CommandPacket commandPacket, int timeoutMillis)
            throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {

        try (SocketChannel channel = SocketChannel.open();
             Selector selector = Selector.open()) {

            connectToServer(channel, selector, timeoutMillis);
            sendCommand(channel, selector, commandPacket, timeoutMillis);
            return receiveResponse(channel, selector, timeoutMillis);
        }
    }

    private void connectToServer(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, int timeoutMillis)
            throws IOException {
        channel.configureBlocking(false);
        channel.connect(new InetSocketAddress(host, port));
        channel.register(selector, SelectionKey.OP_CONNECT);
        waitForChannelReady(channel, selector, SelectionKey.OP_CONNECT, timeoutMillis, "connect");
    }

    private void sendCommand(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, CommandPacket command, int timeoutMillis)
            throws IOException {
        byte[] payload = PacketCodec.serialize(command);

        ByteBuffer lengthBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(4);
        lengthBuffer.putInt(payload.length).flip();
        ByteBuffer payloadBuffer = ByteBuffer.wrap(payload);

        channel.register(selector, SelectionKey.OP_WRITE);

        writeBufferCompletely(channel, selector, lengthBuffer, timeoutMillis, "write length");
        writeBufferCompletely(channel, selector, payloadBuffer, timeoutMillis, "write payload");
    }

    private void writeBufferCompletely(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, ByteBuffer buffer,
                                       int timeoutMillis, String operationName) throws IOException {
        long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis;

        while (buffer.hasRemaining()) {
            long remainingTime = deadline - System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (remainingTime <= 0 || !isChannelReady(selector, remainingTime, SelectionKey.OP_WRITE)) {
                throw new SocketTimeoutException("Timeout while " + operationName);
            }
            channel.write(buffer);
        }
    }

    private ResponsePacket receiveResponse(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, int timeoutMillis)
            throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
        channel.register(selector, SelectionKey.OP_READ);

        ByteBuffer lengthBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(4);
        readBufferCompletely(channel, selector, lengthBuffer, timeoutMillis, "read length");
        int payloadLength = lengthBuffer.flip().getInt();

        validatePayloadLength(payloadLength);

        ByteBuffer payloadBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(payloadLength);
        readBufferCompletely(channel, selector, payloadBuffer, timeoutMillis, "read payload");

        Object decoded = PacketCodec.deserialize(payloadBuffer.array());
        if (!(decoded instanceof ResponsePacket response)) {
            throw new IOException("Server sent unsupported packet type");
        }
        return response;
    }

    private void readBufferCompletely(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, ByteBuffer buffer,
                                      int timeoutMillis, String operationName) throws IOException {
        long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis;

        while (buffer.hasRemaining()) {
            long remainingTime = deadline - System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (remainingTime <= 0 || !isChannelReady(selector, remainingTime, SelectionKey.OP_READ)) {
                throw new SocketTimeoutException("Timeout while " + operationName);
            }
            int bytesRead = channel.read(buffer);
            if (bytesRead < 0) {
                throw new IOException("Server closed connection prematurely");
            }
        }
    }

    private void waitForChannelReady(SocketChannel channel, Selector selector, int interest,
                                     int timeoutMillis, String operationName) throws IOException {
        long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis;

        while (true) {
            if (interest == SelectionKey.OP_CONNECT && channel.finishConnect()) {
                return;
            }

            long remainingTime = deadline - System.currentTimeMillis();
            if (remainingTime <= 0 || !isChannelReady(selector, remainingTime, interest)) {
                throw new SocketTimeoutException("Timeout while " + operationName);
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean isChannelReady(Selector selector, long waitMillis, int interest) throws IOException {
        int readyCount = selector.select(waitMillis);
        if (readyCount == 0) {
            return false;
        }

        Iterator<SelectionKey> keys = selector.selectedKeys().iterator();
        while (keys.hasNext()) {
            SelectionKey key = keys.next();
            keys.remove();

            if (!key.isValid()) continue;

            boolean isReady = (interest == SelectionKey.OP_CONNECT && key.isConnectable())
                    || (interest == SelectionKey.OP_WRITE && key.isWritable())
                    || (interest == SelectionKey.OP_READ && key.isReadable());

            if (isReady) return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private void validatePayloadLength(int length) throws IOException {
        if (length <= 0 || length > MAX_PACKET_BYTES) {
            throw new IOException("Invalid payload length: " + length + " (max: " + MAX_PACKET_BYTES + ")");
        }
    }
}

package client;

import client.commands.CommandDispatcher;
import client.commands.CommandExecutionContext;
import client.ui.ClientUI;
import common.models.Coordinates;
import common.models.MusicBand;
import common.models.MusicGenre;
import common.models.Studio;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileNotFoundException;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.util.Set;

public class ScriptExecutor {
    private final CommandDispatcher dispatcher;
    private final Set<String> runningScripts = new HashSet<>();
    private final ClientUI ui;
    private Scanner activeScanner;

    public ScriptExecutor(ClientCommandRegistry commandRegistry, NonBlockingTcpClient tcpClient, ClientUI ui) {
        this.ui = ui;
        CommandExecutionContext context = new CommandExecutionContext(
                commandRegistry, tcpClient,
                this::readBandFromActiveScript,
                this::executeScript,
                false, true, 1, ui
        );
        this.dispatcher = new CommandDispatcher(context);
    }

    public boolean executeScript(String fileName) {
        if (runningScripts.contains(fileName)) {
            ui.println("Recursion detected for script: " + fileName);
            return true;
        }

        File scriptFile = new File(fileName);
        if (!scriptFile.exists() || !scriptFile.canRead()) {
            ui.println("Script file is unavailable: " + fileName);
            return true;
        }
        runningScripts.add(fileName);
        try (Scanner scanner = new Scanner(scriptFile)) {
            int lineNo = 0;
            while (scanner.hasNextLine()) {
                lineNo++;
                String rawLine = scanner.nextLine().trim();
                if (rawLine.isEmpty() || rawLine.startsWith("#")) continue;
                String[] parts = rawLine.split(" ", 2);
                String commandName = parts[0];
                String argument = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].trim() : "";
                activeScanner = scanner;
                boolean shouldContinue = dispatcher.dispatch(commandName, argument, fileName + ":" + lineNo);
                if (!shouldContinue) return false;
            }
        } catch (FileNotFoundException e) {
            ui.println("Script file not found: " + fileName);
        } finally {
            activeScanner = null;
            runningScripts.remove(fileName);
        }
        return true;
    }

    private MusicBand readBandFromScript(Scanner scanner) {
        String name = scanner.nextLine().trim();
        double x = Double.parseDouble(scanner.nextLine().trim());
        int y = Integer.parseInt(scanner.nextLine().trim());
        long participants = Long.parseLong(scanner.nextLine().trim());
        String singlesLine = scanner.nextLine().trim();
        Integer singles = singlesLine.isEmpty() ? null : Integer.parseInt(singlesLine);
        String dateLine = scanner.nextLine().trim();
        LocalDate establishmentDate = dateLine.isEmpty() ? null : LocalDate.parse(dateLine);
        String genreLine = scanner.nextLine().trim();
        MusicGenre genre = genreLine.isEmpty() ? null : MusicGenre.valueOf(genreLine);
        String studioLine = scanner.nextLine().trim();
        Studio studio = studioLine.isEmpty() ? null : new Studio(studioLine);
        return new MusicBand(name, new Coordinates(x, y), participants, singles, establishmentDate, genre, studio);
    }

    private MusicBand readBandFromActiveScript() {
        if (activeScanner == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Script scanner is not active.");
        return readBandFromScript(activeScanner);
    }
}
